### [1] [Interface-Aware KV Cache Quantization for Dense On-Chip NVM in Long-Context LLM Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05764)
*Jiahao Zheng,Yifan Qin,Xiaobo Sharon Hu,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向片上NVM KV缓存接口的4-bit KV量化方案，牺牲部分软件侧精度，换取更低的读能耗和元数据开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码中，KV缓存读取成为主要内存带宽瓶颈。将量化后的KV缓存放入片上NVM可减少片外传输，但现有KIVI和KVQuant等方法需要额外元数据或稀疏高精度异常值，不适合固定范围转换器后的密集NVM接口。

Method: 采用随机旋转和逐向量归一化，使所有坐标具有相同数值范围；为key和value分别使用共享的固定码本。码本阈值直接映射为读转换器参考电平，无需逐token重配置；反量化仅使用16项查表和一次范数乘加，每向量只保留一个标量元数据。

Result: 在3B到14B模型、最长32k上下文的实验中，4-bit KV缓存可在模拟真实器件噪声下保持可接受精度。相比映射到同一NVM的KIVI和KVQuant，KV读取能耗降低3.1–3.6倍，元数据开销约为KIVI的八分之一；但软件环境中KIVI和KVQuant仍更准确。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心价值不是刷新量化精度纪录，而是将KV量化格式与片上NVM及固定范围转换接口协同设计，从而显著降低KV读取能耗和元数据负担。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the dominant memory bottleneck in long-context large language model (LLM) decoding: every step reads it entirely, so decoding is memory-bandwidth bound. Holding a quantized KV cache in dense on-chip non-volatile memory (NVM) removes the off-chip transfer. Existing KV quantization methods, however, were designed for GPU-style memory systems: KIVI attaches per-group metadata, adding about 25% to the stored KV cache; KVQuant keeps sparse full-precision outliers that a dense array cannot hold in place. This paper examines what these structures cost when the KV cache resides in NVM behind fixed-range converters, and designs a quantization scheme matched to that interface. The architecture stores the quantized KV cache in dense on-chip NVM, uses a small static analog crossbar only for the fixed rotation, and keeps attention in on-chip digital logic. A randomized rotation and per-vector normalization give every coordinate the same range, so one fixed codebook for keys and one for values, each shared across all tokens of the corresponding tensor type, serve the entire KV cache. The codebook thresholds are programmed once as the read converter's reference levels, enabling fixed-range digitization with no per-token converter reconfiguration. Dequantization is a sixteen-entry lookup and one norm multiply; the only per-vector metadata is one scalar, about 3%. Across models from 3B to 14B and contexts to 32k tokens, the four-bit KV cache maintains accuracy under storage and crossbar noise simulated at realistic device levels. KIVI and KVQuant remain more accurate in software; the advantage of our format lies at the memory interface: 3.1-3.6x lower KV read energy than both mapped to the same NVM, and 8x lower metadata overhead than KIVI. The contribution is a KV quantization co-designed with the NVM memory interface rather than a new accuracy record.

</details>


### [2] [GPU-Accelerated Gate-Level Time-Based Power Analysis via Event-Density-Aware Partitioning and Kernel Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05960)
*Weihao Wang,Yikang Ouyang,Hongyuan Liu,Yuzhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出首个面向门级电路的时间域功耗分析GPU加速框架，通过事件密度感知划分和单内核融合功耗计算，在保持高精度的同时显著提升分析速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大规模芯片设计需要细粒度的时间域功耗信息来定位功耗问题和指导优化，但现有门级时间域功耗分析耗时可达数十小时，严重拖慢设计迭代流程。

Method: 作者设计了一种支持高效状态相关功耗查询的新数据结构；针对门之间事件分布不均衡的问题，提出事件密度感知划分策略，按门事件密度分配GPU线程；同时将功耗计算融合为单次kernel调用，减少多kernel间的冗余开销。

Result: 实验表明该方法具有较高精度，相比多线程Synopsys PrimeTime PX实现了最高37.63倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 该框架有效缓解了门级时间域功耗分析在大规模电路中的性能瓶颈，有望显著提升芯片功耗优化流程的效率。

Abstract: Power analysis is crucial in modern chip design flow. Particularly, time-based power analysis can provide fine-grained power consumption information to facilitate the diagnosis of power issues and guide power optimization accordingly. However, it may take tens of hours to conduct time-based power analysis on modern large-scale circuits, which greatly slows down the power optimization flow. In this paper, we present the first GPU-accelerated gate-level time-based power analysis framework. We propose a novel data structure to enable efficient state-dependent power retrieval. To accommodate the imbalanced event distribution across gates, we propose an event-density-aware partitioning strategy that allocates GPU threads based on gate event density. Finally, we fuse the power computation into a single kernel invocation to reduce redundant work in separate kernels. Experimental results show that our proposed framework achieves high accuracy while delivering up to 37.63x end-to-end speedup compared to multi-threaded Synopsys PrimeTime PX.

</details>


### [3] [CMD: An Integrated CGRA Framework with Cluster-Based Distributed Memory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05982)
*Shangkun Li,Cheng Tan,Zeyu Li,Jinming Ge,Jiawei Liang,Hao Yang,Linfeng Du,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种集成的CGRA框架CMD，采用基于簇的分布式内存设计和协同设计的编译工具链，以提升内存密集型内核的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CGRA的刚性内存架构限制了可访问数据内存的图块数量和位置，成为内存密集型内核的性能瓶颈。

Method: 设计了包含内存感知映射器和设计空间探索（DSE）机制的编译器，自动为特定内核寻找最优内存架构。

Result: 实验表明，经过DSE优化的CMD CGRA相比传统CGRA平均加速1.39倍，面积缩减至0.912倍。

Conclusion: CMD框架通过内存架构与编译协同设计，有效提升了CGRA的能效和可配置性。

Abstract: Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Arrays (CGRAs) are a promising solution for achieving high energy efficiency and reconfigurability across various application domains, but their performance is often crippled by rigid memory architectures that limit the number and location of tiles that can access data memory. This creates a significant bottleneck for kernels with intensive memory accesses. To address this, we propose CMD, an integrated CGRA framework featuring cluster-based distributed memory design with a co-designed compilation toolchain. The compiler includes a novel memory-aware mapper and a design space exploration (DSE) mechanism that identifies the optimal memory architecture design for specific kernels. Experimental results show that our post-DSE CMD CGRAs achieve an average speedup of $1.39\times$ over a conventional CGRA while simultaneously reducing the total area to an average of $0.912\times$ of the conventional CGRA.

</details>


### [4] [WaferTrans: Enabling IOMMU-free Distributed Virtual Address Translation for Wafer-scale GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06125)
*Xinru Tang,Jingxiang Hou,Guanghong Wu,Yang Hu,Shouyi Yin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了 WaferTrans，一种无 IOMMU 的 wafer-scale GPU 分布式虚拟地址翻译方案，通过 PTE Presence Consistency 与 PTE Presence Directory 实现片上远程 PTE 定位与查询，消除 CPU-IOMMU 瓶颈，相比 SOTA 平均提升 2.5x 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 wafer-scale GPU 设计仍依赖 CPU-IOMMU 进行远程虚拟地址翻译，该集中机制在 GPU dies 超过数十个时，需通过昂贵的片外层级 traversal 并争抢有限 CPU 资源，成为地址翻译的关键瓶颈。

Method: WaferTrans 引入 PTE Presence Consistency（PTE-PC）轻量级一致性模型，追踪 PTE 插入与移除；每个 GPU 配备 PTE Presence Directory（PPD）定位持有请求 PTE 的 GPU；并利用分布式 PTE-PC 映射与协作查询机制，在保证完整查找覆盖的同时局部化 PTE-PC 维护，实现片上完全自主的远程地址翻译。

Result: 与 SOTA Trans-FW 设计相比，WaferTrans 在平均性能上提升了 2.5 倍。

Conclusion: WaferTrans 通过分布式 PTE-PC 与 PPD 机制，有效消除了 CPU-IOMMU 对 wafer-scale GPU 统一内存的依赖，实现了高效的片上远程翻译。

Abstract: Wafer-scale GPUs (WSGs) provide sufficient on-wafer bandwidth to make near-lossless Unified Memory feasible. However, existing designs still rely on a CPU-IOMMU to translate remote virtual-address accesses. This centralized mechanism scales poorly to tens of GPU dies: translation requests must traverse costly off-wafer hierarchies and contend for limited CPU-side resources, making address translation a critical bottleneck. We propose WaferTrans, an IOMMU-free distributed virtual-address translation design for WSGs. WaferTrans introduces PTE Presence Consistency (PTE-PC), a lightweight consistency model that tracks PTE insertions and removals, and equips each GPU with a PTE Presence Directory (PPD) that locates the GPU holding a requested PTE. It further employs a distributed PTE-PC mapping and a cooperative query mechanism to localize PTE-PC maintenance while preserving complete lookup coverage. Together, these mechanisms enable the GPU array to resolve remote translations within the wafer, eliminating its dependence on the CPU-IOMMU. Compared with the SOTA Trans-FW design, WaferTrans improves performance by 2.5x on average.

</details>


### [5] [From Splats to Silicon: Rethinking Computational Efficiency of 3DGS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06157)
*Minnan Pei,Qiwei Dong,Yihan Zhou,Gang Li,Yuchen Zhu,Wenju Zhao,Zhongtian Long,Siting Wang,Peisong Wang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究通过工作负载中心框架分析了3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）系统效率的各个方面，发现系统收益取决于工作负载减少、阶段粒度匹配以及数据传输和同步成本。研究结合文献分析与实验测量，识别出优化策略对端到端性能的影响，并提出未来系统设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）虽然能够以显式原语表示场景并支持实时新视图合成，但其系统效率在不同场景、视角、渲染路径和平台约束下差异显著。现有研究通过表示方法、算法设计、GPU运行时优化和架构支持来提升效率，但这些改进对应不同的渲染和更新路径点。要将优化成果转化为端到端系统收益，需要追踪每个优化如何改变高斯选择、屏幕空间工作、数据移动和各阶段的时间开销。

Method: 该研究采用工作负载中心框架，将表示学、算法研究、GPU运行时和硬件架构相连接。通过文献分析补充，结合选定实现的复现测量和受控GPU性能分析，建立工作负载计数与阶段时间及内存流量之间的关系。研究重点关注工作负载减少是否能有效传递到下游执行，以及各阶段粒度是否匹配，以及数据传输、同步、缓存结果、梯度和优化器数据的成本。

Result: 研究发现系统收益取决于工作负载减少是否能有效传递到下游执行、阶段粒度是否匹配，以及数据传输、同步、缓存结果、梯度和优化器数据的成本。优化策略的效果因其作用在渲染管线的不同位置而异，单纯的算法或硬件优化可能无法带来整体性能提升。

Conclusion: 本研究强调需要更一致的评估标准，在渲染质量约束下进行系统评估，并明确未来系统设计的关键方向。通过工作负载中心的方法，研究揭示了优化策略与实际性能之间的复杂关系，为后续3DGS系统设计提供了理论指导和实践参考。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) represents scenes with explicit primitives and supports real-time novel-view synthesis, yet its system efficiency varies substantially across scenes, viewpoints, rendering paths, and platform constraints. Existing studies pursue efficiency through representation and algorithm design, GPU runtime optimization, and architectural support, but their reported gains correspond to different points along the rendering and update paths. Connecting these indicators to end-to-end system benefit requires tracing how each optimization changes Gaussian selection, screen-space work, data movement, and stage or frame time. We therefore use a workload-centric framework to connect representation and algorithm research, GPU runtimes, and hardware architectures and to identify recurring workload patterns. We complement literature analysis with reproduced measurements and controlled GPU profiling of selected implementations, relating workload counts to stage time and memory traffic. Together, these comparisons show that system gains depend on workload reductions reaching downstream execution, granularity matching each stage, and the cost of data transfers, synchronization, and cached results, gradients, and optimizer data. Building on these findings, we discuss more consistent evaluation under rendering-quality constraints and identify key directions for future system design.

</details>


### [6] [Towards Enabling Distance-Based Memory Addressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06270)
*Ananth Krishna Prasad,Rajeev Balasubramonian,Mahdi Nazm Bojnordi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据并行硬软件协同机制，通过轻量级原语实现全向量空间的快速近似暴力搜索，并将剪枝作为后处理步骤，在百万和十亿级工作负载上实现近完美召回率，同时性能和能效提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维向量数据集中的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）受限于带宽和维度灾难，传统索引依赖预处理剪枝，但会导致带宽效率低下或搜索质量下降。

Method: 提出新算法简化相似度计算，利用轻量级原语进行数据并行暴力搜索；构建内存系统执行每数据点距离度量生成，将剪枝作为后处理；提供软件支持用户控制。

Result: 在代表性工作负载上实现近完美召回率，相比最先进算法方法在百万和十亿级规模上性能和能效提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该机制通过后处理剪枝有效解决带宽瓶颈，兼顾搜索质量和效率，为大规模 ANNS 提供高效解决方案。

Abstract: Approximate Nearest-Neighbor Search (ANNS) in high dimensional vector datasets is an application of significant prevalence across different AI applications. However, such an operation is significantly bandwidth limited at large workingset sizes owing to the curse of dimensionality. Traditional indices used to accelerate ANNS rely on search-space pruning as a preprocessing step to alleviate such bandwidth requirement, but such optimization occurs either at the cost of increased bandwidth-inefficiency and/or degradation of search quality. This paper proposes a data-parallel hardware/software mechanism for performing large-scale similarity search in-memory. We propose a novel algorithm to simplify the computation requirement for similarity search across various distance metrics through lightweight primitives to perform a fast and approximate data-parallel brute-force search on the entire vector space. We further build a memory system capable of executing the required operations to generate a distance metric per datapoints, which is then used to enable pruning as a post-processing step. We offer adequate software support for user control over the proposed system. By enabling such search-space pruning as a post-processing step, we achieve near-perfect recall across representative workloads while achieving orders of magnitude performance and energy improvement over state-of-the-art algorithmic approaches on million and billion-scale workloads.

</details>


### [7] [BLINK: Batch Normalization-based Integrity Checkpoints for In-Situ Detection and Mitigation of Diverse Weight Corruptions in DNN Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06781)
*Marzia Khan,Akul Malhotra,Sumeet Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了BLINK，一种基于批量归一化（BN）的片上实时检测与缓解方案，旨在应对安全关键AI硬件中的各种权重损坏（如随机故障、局部故障和对抗性位翻转攻击）。它通过持续感知激活统计偏移来检测问题，并在同一次前向传播中重新居中受影响层以恢复准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键场景下的AI硬件面临老化、软错误、硬故障及对抗性攻击（如PBFA）等威胁，这些因素会损坏存储权重，导致模型产生高置信度但错误的预测，因此亟需一种高效的片上检测与缓解机制。

Method: BLINK通过持续感知批量归一化（BN）激活统计的偏移来检测权重损坏。它分为两个阶段：（1）离线预表征激活偏移与推理精度下降的关系；（2）片上运行时检测与缓解。一旦检测到损坏，它会在同一次前向传播中将受影响的层重新居中到干净的参考值。该方法完全自主，无需主机通信或暂停操作。若残余偏移表明精度低于设定阈值，监控程序会中止推理。

Result: 在ResNet-20/50和MobileNetV2（CIFAR-10/100）上的评估显示，BLINK对各类权重损坏的检测精度超过99%。它能显著恢复模型精度：在0.5%随机位翻转下将精度从10%提升至85.88%；在局部故障下恢复至84%；在PBFA攻击下从随机猜测水平恢复至80%-83%。硬件开销极低，延迟增加小于2%，计算开销仅增0.53%。

Conclusion: BLINK提供了一种高效、自主的片上权重损坏检测与缓解方案，具有极高的检测精度和显著的精度恢复能力，且硬件开销极低，非常适合资源受限的安全关键AI应用场景。

Abstract: In safety-critical deployments, AI hardware must remain reliable against a broad spectrum of threats such as aging, soft errors, hard faults, and adversarial attacks (e.g. progressive bit flip attack (PBFA)). All of these corrupt stored weights while the chip keeps producing confident but inaccurate predictions. Detecting and mitigating such weight perturbations is crucial for safety-critical platforms. To that end, we propose BLINK, an on-chip batch normalization (BN)-based on-the-fly detection and mitigation approach, which is based on continual sensing of the shift in the activation statistics, and targets a wide variety of weight corruptions (random and localized faults as well as adversarial bit flips). BLINK operates in two phases: (1) off-line pre-characterization of the relationship of the activation shifts with inference accuracy drop, and (2) on-chip runtime detection and mitigation of weight corruptions. Upon detection, the flagged layer is re-centered to bring it closer to its stored clean reference within the same forward pass. BLINK is fully autonomous, eliminating the need for host communication, operation halts, or access to fine-tuning data. If the residual shift after mitigation indicates that accuracy has fallen below a user-set floor, a held-out watcher aborts the inference. Evaluated on ResNet-20/50 and MobileNetV2 for CIFAR-10/100, BLINK detects harmful corruptions with >99% precision across all fault types. Further, it recovers accuracy from 10% to 85.88% under 0.5% random bit flips (Resnet-50/CIFAR-10), up to 84% for localized faults (MobileNetV2/CIFAR-10), and from random-guess accuracy to 80%-83% under PBFA (ResNet-20/CIFAR-10). Hardware overhead estimates indicate that BLINK incurs negligible costs, with less than a 2% increase in latency and only a 0.53% increase in computation overhead.

</details>


### [8] [Hardware Trojan Threats to Multi-Chiplet Photonic Neural Network Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06796)
*Sudeep Pasricha*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨多芯片光子神经网络加速器面临的硬件木马威胁，从机密性、完整性、可用性三个维度分析安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多芯片光子神经网络加速器结合光子计算、通信和异构芯片集成，虽具备可扩展和节能优势，但其分布式架构和依赖第三方芯片引入显著硬件安全风险。

Method: 通过威胁建模和安全分析，从机密性、完整性、可用性三个维度系统评估硬件木马对MCPNAs的潜在攻击面。

Result: 识别出分布式架构和第三方芯片集成导致的多种硬件木马攻击向量，揭示了在三个安全维度下的具体漏洞和潜在影响。

Conclusion: 针对MCPNAs的硬件木马威胁，需开发跨层级的安全验证和缓解机制，保障光子神经网络加速器的可信部署。

Abstract: Multi-chiplet photonic neural network accelerators (MCPNAs) combine photonic computation, photonic communica-tion, and heterogeneous chiplet integration to enable scalable and energy-efficient AI acceleration. However, their distributed archi-tecture and reliance on third-party chiplets introduce significant hardware security risks. This paper examines Hardware Trojan (HT) threats to MCPNAs across three dimensions: confidentiality, integrity, and availability.

</details>


### [9] [NOVA-CIM: Noise- and Correlation-Tolerant Stochastic Interfaces for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07059)
*Jiachen Ren,Wenshuai Yao,Haobo Liu,Xincheng Feng,Chenxi Hu,Zhengwu Liu,Kechao Tang,Wenyong Zhou,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: NOVA-CIM replaces noisy multi-bit ADC readout with random-reference 1-bit sensing and lightweight counting, enabling robust analog CIM inference with minimal accuracy loss even under dynamic read noise and input correlations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional analog CIM relies on high‑precision ADCs that directly quantize noisy column currents, making inference accuracy sensitive to analog read noise, row scaling, and ADC precision. A noise‑ and correlation‑tolerant stochastic interface is needed to achieve robust, energy‑efficient acceleration.

Method: Propose NOVA‑CIM: (1) convert column currents into comparison probabilities using random reference levels; (2) perform 1‑bit stochastic sensing and accumulate results via lightweight counting; (3) leverage temporal averaging to suppress zero‑mean dynamic read noise; (4) analyze how spatial input bitstream correlation affects current variance without introducing first‑order MAC bias.

Result: Robustness analysis confirms noise suppression via averaging and that input correlation increases variance but not MAC bias. MAC‑level experiments and ViT‑Base end‑to‑end evaluation show: under read noise, Top‑1 accuracy stays at 84.48 % (baseline 84.51 % bfloat16); under stochastic number generator reuse, MAC bias remains near zero while RMSE and stochastic cross‑correlation rise as predicted.

Conclusion: NOVA‑CIM provides a practical, low‑overhead stochastic interface that drastically reduces dependence on high‑resolution ADCs, maintains high inference accuracy under realistic noise conditions, and scales robustly with correlated inputs, making analog CIM viable for production‑grade AI accelerators.

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient model acceleration, but its reliance on ADC-based readout, which directly quantizes noisy column currents, makes inference accuracy highly sensitive to analog read noise, active-row scaling, and ADC precision. In this paper, we present NOVA-CIM, a noise- and correlation-tolerant stochastic interface for analog CIM by replacing multi-bit ADC readout with random-reference 1-bit sensing and reconstructing results through lightweight counting. By converting column currents into comparison probabilities, this probability-domain readout averages zero-mean dynamic read noise over stochastic samples while reducing dependence on high-resolution ADCs. We provide a unified robustness analysis showing that dynamic read noise is suppressed through temporal averaging and that spatial input-bitstream correlation increases instantaneous current variance rather than introducing first-order MAC bias. MAC-level experiments and end-to-end evaluation on ViT-Base validate the analysis: under read noise, Top-1 accuracy remains 84.48% near the 84.51% bfloat16 (BF16) baseline; under stochastic number generator (SNG) reuse, MAC bias stays near zero while root-mean-square error (RMSE) and stochastic cross-correlation (SCC) grow as predicted.

</details>


### [10] [Enabling High-Bandwidth Flash for Generative Recommendation Serving with Write-Aware KV Cache Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07175)
*Danni Peng,Kai Wu,Tianyu Zuo,Pengfei Xia,Hui Zang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HBF provides high capacity and near-HBM bandwidth for GR serving, but LRU cache policy causes excessive writes that wear out flash quickly; an admission‑controlled LRU‑K policy reduces writes, extending HBF lifetime while preserving throughput.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flash endurance becomes a bottleneck in HBF‑based generative recommendation systems because conventional LRU KV‑cache management generates high write traffic on every cache miss.

Method: We propose a write‑aware KV‑cache policy using admission‑controlled LRU‑K, develop an analytical model to predict serving performance, write traffic, and HBF lifetime, and evaluate it across various memory systems and GR workloads.

Result: HBF‑based systems achieve 3.8–4.7× higher throughput than HBM‑only systems; with LRU‑K (K=10) HBF lifetime increases from about one year under LRU to over six years, while throughput remains comparable or slightly better.

Conclusion: Adopting a write‑aware KV‑cache admission policy is crucial for sustainable, high‑throughput HBF‑based generative recommendation serving.

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) systems increasingly leverage user-level KV cache reuse to avoid recomputing long user histories. However, the growing KV cache capacity and bandwidth requirements introduce new challenges for memory system. High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) provides a promising solution by offering substantially higher capacity than HBM while approaching HBM-class read bandwidth, enabling larger scale KV cache retention and improved serving throughput. Yet conventional Least-Recently-Used (LRU) KV cache management tightly couples KV cache writes with cache misses, generating excessive write traffic that rapidly exhausts flash endurance. In this work, we evaluate a write-aware KV cache policy based on admission-controlled LRU-K for HBF-based GR serving. By filtering low-reuse users before cache admission, LRU-K decouples KV cache writes from misses and significantly reduces unnecessary writes. We develop an analytical model to characterize GR serving performance, KV cache write traffic, and HBF lifetime, and evaluate performance across diverse memory systems and GR workloads. Our results show that HBF-based systems achieve 3.8 to 4.7 times higher throughput than HBM-only systems. Moreover, LRU-K extends HBF lifetime from about one year under conventional LRU to over six years with a moderate K=10, while maintaining comparable or even slightly improved throughput. These results highlight the importance of write aware KV cache policy for sustainable HBF-based GR serving.

</details>


### [11] [A 28nm 27,648-Spin Multichip Digital Ising Accelerator with Pegasus Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07907)
*Tong Wu,Atharva Raut,Ian Khor,Ziyad Alswaidan,Ting-Yu Lee,Hongchang Kuang,Navid Anjum Aadit,Anand Raju,Dhruv Chinmay,Siddharth Das,Ken Mai,Kerem Camsari,Tathagata Srimani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 我们提出了一款基于28nm工艺的数字Ising加速器，集成27,648个自旋于四片芯片，采用时分复用的自旋更新阵列配合局部SRAM和调度的芯间传输，实现41.5G updates/s，每更新1.2pJ。支持度-15 Pegasus连接和10b系数，提升了原生连接性和精度，可用于优化和概率逻辑，四片芯片植入MaxCut实例在3.3μs内求解27,069个节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过提升自旋更新速率和能效，突破现有多芯片退火器在连接度和精度上的限制，实现高性能组合优化与概率逻辑求解。

Method: 采用28nm数字Ising架构，时分复用自旋更新阵列配合局部SRAM，调度芯间传输；引入度-15 Pegasus拓扑和10b系数，可编程耦合支持优化与概率逻辑运算。

Result: 实现41.5G updates/s，能效1.2pJ/update；四片芯片植入MaxCut在3.3μs内求解27,069节点；较度-8、5b多芯片退火器在连接性和精度上显著提升。

Conclusion: 该加速器在能效、吞吐量和连接度上均取得显著突破，为大规模Ising模型和组合优化问题提供高效硬件支持。

Abstract: We present a 28nm digital Ising accelerator with 27,648 spins across four chips. A time-multiplexed spin-update array with local SRAM and scheduled interchip transfers delivers 41.5G updates/s at 1.2pJ/update. Degree-15 Pegasus connectivity and 10b coefficients increase native connectivity and precision over degree-8, 5b multichip annealers. Programmable couplings support optimization and probabilistic logic, with a four-chip planted-MaxCut trace reaching the solution for 27,069 nodes in 3.3$μ$s.

</details>


### [12] [Routing Dense Layouts with History-Aware Offline Reinforcement Learning using LSTM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08232)
*Afsara Khan,Austin Rovinski*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 一种基于历史感知的离线强化学习策略，结合轻量级LSTM和额外路由器特征，显著提高了高密度设计下详细布线的收敛性，平均减少92%的设计规则违规并降低10%运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 详细布线因设计规则复杂性增加而成为物理设计中的主要运行时间瓶颈，现有基于强化学习的动态成本选择方法在高密度设计下表现不佳，难以解决持续违规问题。

Method: 提出历史感知离线RL策略，采用保守Q学习，引入轻量级LSTM架构保持序列上下文，并利用路由器现成特征预测迭代成本权重，可无缝集成到任何基于成本的路由器中。

Result: 在保留的密度和调整设置（包括由密集布局和低引导质量引起的困难操作点）上评估，与顶级公开基线相比，平均减少92%的设计规则违规（DRVs），同时运行时间减少10%。

Conclusion: 该策略通过最小化管道更改即可集成到现有路由器，有效应对多种密度和引导质量条件，显著提升布线收敛性和效率。

Abstract: Detailed routing remains a dominant runtime bottleneck in physical design due to increasing complexity of design rules. Modern routers can struggle to resolve persistent violations under dense operating conditions. While recent work leverages reinforcement learning (RL) to dynamically select costs for each routing iteration, we find that this technique struggles with high-density designs where routing solutions are significantly harder. To address this, we present a history-aware offline RL policy which predicts iterative cost weights in these dense regimes to improve convergence across placement densities by utilizing readily available features from the router. Our policy uses conservative Q-learning similarly to prior work; however, our key insight is that addition of a lightweight LSTM architecture and additional features can retain sequence context and improve routing convergence across multiple densities and route guide qualities. Our policy can be integrated into any cost-based router with minimal pipeline changes, as it does not interfere with the core search algorithm. We evaluate our policy on held-out density and adjustment settings, including difficult operating points induced by dense placement and low guide quality. Our policy reduces design rule violations (DRVs) by an average of 92% over the top public baseline while simultaneously reducing runtime by 10%.

</details>


### [13] [PENDA: An Efficient Processing Element via Norm-of-Difference for Deep Learning Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08424)
*Kai-Chieh Hsu,Tian-Sheuan Chang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 论文提出 PENDA，通过余弦定理将内积中的乘法转化为平方差运算，在不牺牲精确性的前提下提升深度学习加速器处理单元阵列的硬件效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内积计算是深度学习模型计算成本的主要来源，现有加速方法多依赖近似计算，可能降低模型准确率。因此，需要在保持计算精确的同时优化硬件面积、能耗和时序性能。

Method: PENDA 利用余弦定理重新表达乘法运算，将传统乘累加单元替换为基于范数差的计算单元，从而减少乘法器带来的硬件开销。

Result: 在深度学习加速器处理单元阵列中，该方法相比传统设计实现了 11%~36% 的面积降低、5%~48% 的能耗降低，以及 11%~19% 的时钟周期降低。

Conclusion: PENDA 为深度学习硬件加速提供了一种保持精确性的内积计算优化方案，能够显著改善处理单元阵列的面积、能耗和速度表现。

Abstract: Inner product computation dominates the computational cost of deep learning models; thus, accelerating this primitive is key to improving hardware efficiency. However, most existing techniques rely on approximations, which can degrade model accuracy. To preserve exactness while optimizing hardware, this paper presents PENDA (processing element via norm-of-difference architecture), which leverages the law of cosines to recast multiplications as squared-difference operations. Replacing multiply-accumulate units with the proposed norm-of-difference units yields 11~36%, 5~48%, and 11~19% reductions in area, energy, and clock period, respectively, for the PE array of a deep learning accelerator.

</details>


### [14] [FlexSpIM: An Event-Based Digital Compute-In-Memory Accelerator with Flexible Operand Resolution and Layer-Wise Hybrid Stationarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08446)
*Nicolas Chauvaux,Adrian Kneip,Charlotte Frenkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexSpIM，一种支持任意操作数分辨率和形状的数字CIM架构，通过统一存储权重和神经元状态，实现层级混合权重输出定数据流，显著降低数据搬运开销，并在40nm工艺原型上实现了高能效与吞吐量，并在IBM DVS数据集上获得95.8%精度，相比固定定点方案实现最高45%能耗和52%延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIM加速器在电路和系统层面的有限灵活性限制了其在多样化工负载上的部署，尤其是脉冲神经网络(SNN)的边缘视觉应用需要更高的灵活性与效率。

Method: 提出了FlexSpIM数字CIM架构，支持任意操作数分辨率和形状的统一存储，用于权重和膜电位。通过层级混合权重与输出定数据流，最大化操作数复用，减少芯内芯外数据搬运。支持位分辨率重配置。

Result: 40nm CMOS工艺测量结果显示，FlexSpIM实现了1bit归一化能效效率和更高吞吐量，相比先前固定精度数字CIM-based SNN加速器。在IBM DVS手势数据集上实现95.8%精度，相比固定定点方法在大规模系统中实现最高45%能耗和52%延迟降低。

Conclusion: FlexSpIM通过统一存储与混合数据流，打破了CIM加速器的灵活性瓶颈，在能效、延迟和精度之间取得了有竞争力的性能，展现出在边缘视觉SNN应用中的广泛部署潜力。

Abstract: Compute-in-memory (CIM) accelerators for spiking neural networks (SNNs) offer a promising solution for achieving $μ$s-level inference latency and ultra-low energy in edge vision applications. However, their limited flexibility at both circuit and system levels restricts their deployment across diverse workloads. This work introduces FlexSpIM, a digital CIM architecture supporting arbitrary operand resolution and shape within a unified storage for weights and neuron states (i.e., membrane potentials). These circuit-level capabilities enable a layer-level hybrid weight- and output-stationary dataflow, maximizing operand reuse and reducing costly on- and off-chip data movement during SNN execution. Measurement results from a fabricated FlexSpIM prototype in 40-nm CMOS demonstrate competitive 1-bit-normalized energy efficiency and higher throughput compared with prior fixed-precision digital CIM-based SNN accelerators, while providing bitwise resolution reconfiguration. Evaluated on the IBM DVS gesture dataset, FlexSpIM achieves 95.8% accuracy while enabling up to 45% energy and 52% latency reductions in large-scale systems compared with fixed stationarity approaches.

</details>


### [15] [HDA-MoE: Hybrid Parallelism and Dynamic, Adaptive Scheduling for Mixture-of-Experts with 3D Near-Memory Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08682)
*Haochen Huang,Shuzhang Zhong,Shengxuan Qiu,Zhe Zhang,Shuangchen Li,Cong Li,Dimin Niu,Hongzhong Zheng,Guangyu Sun,Runsheng Wang,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出HDA-MoE框架，结合离线混合并行映射与在线自适应调度，在3D近内存处理架构上高效执行混合专家模型，显著降低通信开销并提升计算利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽然能以较低计算成本获得高容量，但在3D NMP系统中会导致内存带宽需求增加以及传统并行策略（TP、EP）通信开销大、负载不均的问题，动态路由进一步加剧调度难度。

Method: HDA-MoE 包含两部分：离线阶段使用混合并行映射算法将专家和张量划分到多个计算单元，以最小化跨节点通信；在线阶段采用动态自适应调度器根据实时路由决策和负载情况重新分配任务，以平衡计算并隐藏通信延迟。

Result: 实验表明，相较于传统并行方案，HDA-MoE 在不同工作负载下实现了 1.1×–3.4× 超越 TP、1.1×–1.5× 超越 EP、1.1×–3.7× 超越混合 TP-EP 基线以及 1.1×–1.3× 超越之前的 HD-MoE 的加速比。

Conclusion: 通过统一离线映射与在线自适应调度，HDA-MoE 有效缓解了 3D NMP 架构上 MoE 推理的通信瓶颈和负载不均问题，证明了混合并行与动态调度相结合的可行性，为未来大规模 MoE 在新兴存算一体平台上的高效部署提供了参考方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have become a key technique for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs), enabling high model capacity with reduced computational cost. However, this efficiency comes at the expense of increased memory capacity and bandwidth demands. Recent 3D Near-Memory Processing (NMP) architectures, which vertically integrate memory and compute through hybrid bonding, provide high internal bandwidth and energy efficiency, making them attractive for accelerating MoE inference. Nevertheless, the distributed memory and compute organization of NMP systems introduces new challenges for mapping MoE workloads. Existing parallelization strategies, such as Tensor Parallelism (TP) and Expert Parallelism (EP), suffer from either high communication costs or unbalanced computation utilization, leading to inferior efficiency. In addition, the dynamic routing behavior of MoE models further complicates efficient deployment. To address these challenges, we present HDA-MoE, a framework that optimizes MoE execution on NMP architectures through hybrid parallel deployment and runtime scheduling. HDA-MoE integrates an offline hybrid parallel mapping algorithm with an online dynamic and adaptive scheduling mechanism to reduce communication overhead while improving computation utilization. Experimental results show that HDA-MoE achieves a speedup of 1.1x--3.4x over TP, 1.1x--1.5x over EP, 1.1x--3.7x over the Hybrid TP-EP compute-balanced baseline, and 1.1x--1.3x over HD-MoE. Source code is available at https://github.com/PKU-SEC-Lab/HDA-MoE-TCAD26.

</details>

### [16] [CaseWeaver: A Multi-Agent Framework for Multimodal Virtual Clinical Case Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05480)
*Jierui Qu,Jiachuan Peng,Lin Li,Kyle Lam,Jianing Qiu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为CaseWeaver的多智能体框架，用于生成具有一致患者背景、连贯疾病轨迹和相关多模态证据的虚拟临床病例。该框架以时间线锚定的潜在临床病例图(LCCG)为核心，通过模态智能体生成临床记录、实验结果、生理信号和医学图像等证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断依赖来自同一患者在疾病过程中收集的一致多模态数据，但由于收集成本高、缺失模态、系统碎片化和纵向随访困难等原因，难以大规模获取。现有的合成数据方法多局限于单一模态或基于双模态的报告级生成，缺乏在完整临床病例级别上构建具有一致性和关联性的合成数据。

Method: 提出以时间线锚定的潜在临庍病例图(LCCG)为基础的多智能体框架CaseWeaver。LCCG在共享的患者级别表示中组织患者背景、潜在疾病状态、临床事件和预期观察结果。模态智能体使用受约束的观察子图和临床协议生成包括临床记录、实验室结果、生理信号和医学图像在内的证据。使用校准后的AgentClinic协议评估临床可推断性，并采用虚拟病例多样性(VCD)分数评估病例多样性。

Result: CaseWeaver在两个评估指标上均优于通用模型和基于智能体的工作流程基线方法，在生成更多样化和连贯的多模态虚拟临床病例方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: CaseWeaver为生成一致且多样化的虚拟临床病例提供了一种有效方法，有望在降低数据获取成本、提升临床AI模型训练数据质量等方面发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis relies on consistent multimodal data collected from the same patient throughout the disease course, yet such data are difficult to acquire at scale because of collection costs, missing modalities, fragmented systems, and longitudinal follow-ups. Existing synthetic-data approaches largely focus on individual modalities or vision-language dual modalities at report-level generation. Little work has been done to construct synthetic data with consistent patient backgrounds, coherent disease trajectories, and interrelated modality-specific evidence at a complete clinical case level. We introduce CaseWeaver, a multi-agent framework built around a timeline-anchored Latent Clinical Case Graph (LCCG). The LCCG organizes patient context, latent disease states, clinical events, and expected observations in a shared patient-level representation. Modality-agents use scoped observation subgraphs and clinical protocols to generate evidence including clinical records, laboratory results, physiological signals, and medical images. We evaluate clinical inferability using a calibrated AgentClinic protocol and case diversity using Virtual Case Diversity (VCD) score. CaseWeaver outperformed general-model and agentic-workflow baselines on both metrics, producing more diverse and coherent multimodal virtual clinical cases.

</details>


### [17] [DART: A DAG-Based Reputation and Incentive Framework via Blockchain-Enabled Governance for Trustworthy LLM Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05529)
*Manoj Kumala,Xinyun Liua,Ronghua Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DART introduces a DAG・based reputation and incentive framework that merges centralized orchestration with blockchain‑enabled decentralized governance to make LLM‑driven multi‑agent systems trustworthy, resilient, and accountable. It dynamically selects agents by balancing task fit, reputation, and load, continuously updates trust via verifiable on‑chain evidence, and uses multi‑factor incentives and IPFS‑linked smart contracts to enforce accountability. Evaluations show state‑of‑the‑art reasoning performance, fast full‑stack execution, and robust containment of malicious agents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM‑based MAS rely on centralized orchestration and lack formal verification of agent reliability, participation, and system‑level alignment, leaving open environments vulnerable to uncooperative or malicious agents.

Method: DART unifies DAG workflow orchestration, capability & reputation‑aware task allocation, dynamic behavior updates, multi‑factor incentives, and smart‑contract accountability with IPFS storage. Agent selection balances task alignment, historical reputation, and workload in real time; post‑execution behavioral evidence on‑chain continuously calibrates trust and future participation probability.

Result: Key quantitative results: • 93.6% % Pass@1 on GSM8K; • full‑stack app built in 142 s with two agents, beating centralized baselines; • longitudinal trials (5×150 rounds): mean task success 93.33%± 2.26%, output quality 0.9357± 0.0117, retry rate 0.2307± 0.0816, allocation delay 1.1153± 0.0408 s; • malicious‑agent isolation: 99.3% output containment, system success recovered to 99.8%. 

Conclusion: Coupling reputation management, incentive mechanisms, DAG‑based coordination, and verifiable blockchain governance creates adaptive, accountable multi‑agent collaboration that significantly improves reliability and resilience against malicious behavior.

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) predominantly rely on centralized orchestration and lack formal verification mechanisms for agent reliability, participation, and system-level behavioral alignment. These shortcomings leave open environments severely vulnerable to uncooperative or malicious agents. This work proposes DART, a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG)-based reputation and incentive regulation framework for trustworthy multi-agent collaboration, combining centralized operational orchestration with blockchain-enabled decentralized governance and accountability. DART unifies DAG workflow orchestration, capability and reputation-aware task allocation, dynamic behavior updates, multi-factor incentives, and smart contract accountability paired with IPFS storage. Under this paradigm, agent selection dynamically balances task alignment, historical reputation, and workload, while post-execution behavioral evidence continuously calibrates agent trust and the probability of future participation. Evaluated across four axes, DART achieves 93.6% Pass@1 on GSM8K and builds a full-stack application in 142 s using two agents, outperforming centralized baselines. Across five independent 150-round longitudinal trials, Full DART achieves a mean task success rate of 93.33 +/- 2.26%, output quality of 0.9357 +/- 0.0117, retry rate of 0.2307 +/- 0.0816, and allocation delay of 1.1153 +/- 0.0408 s, consistently outperforming its ablated configurations DART isolates persistent and intermittent malicious agents, obtaining a 99.3% output containment rate and restoring system success to 99.8%. These results demonstrate the potential of coupling reputation, incentives, DAG-based coordination, and verifiable blockchain-enabled governance to support adaptive and accountable multi-agent collaboration.

</details>


### [18] [SimTIO: A Simulation-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Compositional Traffic Intervention Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05740)
*Shuyang Li,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出SimTIO，一种基于仿真约束的多智能体大语言模型框架，用于在交通拥堵诊断后组合并选择可执行干预方案，同时控制对全局网络性能和安全约束的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通分析师需要把识别出的瓶颈转化为可执行措施，但局部优化可能带来网络级延误增加、相邻道路溢出、吞吐量下降等问题，因此需要一种受约束、可验证、基于反馈的方案生成与选择机制。

Method: SimTIO先在SUMO中运行未干预场景，冻结并识别10个主要瓶颈边；随后用grounded sampler生成信号控制、走廊速度、保持需求的路由等操作；三个专业代理根据仿真反馈从经验证的变异目录中选择单参数改进；兼容操作被组合后重新仿真，以测量交互影响；最终在多个约束下最小化瓶颈时间损失，并保留无操作场景作为安全兜底。

Result: 在15个案例、5个城市网络、3个合成需求种子和每场景2400次OD出行的实验设置下，SimTIO平均降低Top-10瓶颈时间损失9.18%，降低全网延误2.78%；在相同7次仿真预算下，其找到可行改进方案的比率为86.7%，高于随机搜索的73.3%和确定性启发式的80.0%。不过，Top-10瓶颈改善差异未达到统计显著。

Conclusion: 研究表明，大语言模型适合作为受约束、仿真反馈引导的本地搜索算子，而不是直接决策者。交通干预应由可执行工具、微观仿真和明确安全约束共同验证与最终把关。

Abstract: Traffic analysts must translate diagnosed bottlenecks into executable interventions without allowing local improvements to degrade network-wide performance. This study presents SimTIO, a simulation-grounded multi-agent large language model framework for composing and selecting traffic interventions under explicit operational constraints. SimTIO first simulates an unmodified SUMO scenario to identify a baseline-frozen set of ten bottleneck edges. A grounded sampler then initializes signal-control, corridor-speed, and demand-preserving routing actions, while three specialist agents use measured simulation feedback to select one-parameter refinements from validator-confirmed mutation catalogs. Compatible actions are combined and re-simulated so that their interaction effects are measured rather than inferred. Final selection minimizes bottleneck time loss while constraining network-wide delay, neighboring-road spillover, throughput loss, and teleport events, with the unmodified scenario retained as a no-operation guard. Across 15 cases covering five U.S. urban networks, three synthetic-demand seeds, and 2,400 origin-destination trips per scenario, SimTIO reduced Top-10 bottleneck time loss by an average of 9.18 percent and network-wide delay by 2.78 percent. It found a feasible improving plan in 86.7 percent of cases, compared with 73.3 percent for grounded random search and 80.0 percent for a deterministic heuristic under the same seven-simulation budget, although the differences in Top-10 improvement were not statistically significant. These results support using LLMs as constrained, feedback-guided local search operators while reserving final decision authority for executable tools, microscopic simulation, and explicit safety constraints.

</details>


### [19] [Adaptive stabilization of a leaderless bearing-constrained formation with disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06355)
*Minh Hoang Trinh,Chuong Van Nguyen,Quoc Van Tran,Tuynh Van Pham*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对在未知上界有界连续干扰下的单积分器智能体，研究了具有双向方位约束的移动目标编队调节与维持问题。通过结合自适应滑模控制与光滑自适应控制，实现了编队的稳定与抗干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际编队控制中常存在时变且上界未知的干扰，导致目标编队呈时变特性。传统方法难以在干扰信息不完全已知的情况下保证编队的精确稳定，且滑模控制存在的抖振问题限制了其实际应用，因此需要设计既能抗干扰又能减缓抖振的控制律。

Method: 首先，针对存在匹配干扰的系统，设计自适应滑模控制律，在不依赖干扰上界先验知识的情况下，实现移动目标编队的一致全局渐近稳定和干扰抑制。其次，为减轻滑模控制的抖振，进一步提出光滑自适应控制律，保证编队系统的一致全局有界性。最后通过仿真验证。

Result: 自适应滑模控制律成功实现了移动编队的一致全局渐近稳定并有效抑制了匹配干扰；光滑自适应控制律在保证编队一致全局有界的同时，显著缓解了控制信号的抖振现象。仿真结果支持了上述理论分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应控制策略有效解决了未知干扰下移动目标方位约束编队的鲁棒调节问题，在保证编队控制精度的同时，兼顾了抗干扰性能与减轻控制抖振的需求。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the problem of regulating and maintaining a target formation characterized by a set of bidirectional bearing constraints under disturbances. The agents in the formation are modeled by single integrators with bounded continuous disturbances of which the upper bound is unavailable for the control design. Due to the time-varying disturbances, the target formation is time-varying. We propose adaptive sliding mode control laws to uniformly globally asymptotically stabilizes the moving target formation and reject the matched disturbances. In addition, to alleviate chattering phenomena from sliding mode control, smooth adaptive control laws are then designed to guarantee uniform global boundedness of the desired formation. Finally, simulation results are given to support the analysis.

</details>


### [20] [CAPMAS: Capability-Based Delegation of Privileges in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06500)
*Rasmus Moorits Veski,Rachid Guerraoui,David Froelicher*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CAPMAS 是一种用于多智能体系统中安全、高效端到端查询执行的新型架构。它通过结合基于对比学习的语义范围界定管道与基于 Macaroon 的令牌，实现了离线、防篡改的跨代理委托，并强制执行严格的最小权限保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在两类主要缺陷：一类直接向代理传播用户身份，导致问责模糊并放大了 AI 非确定性带来的持久过度权限风险；另一类依赖与中央身份和访问管理（IAM）提供商的持续同步，引入了较高的延迟和通信开销。

Method: CAPMAS 创新性地结合了基于对比学习的语义范围界定管道（在执行前将自然语言查询映射到有界权限集）与表达性强、支持离线防篡改且能跨代理实现单调权限缩减的 Macaroon 令牌。通过将认证和委托强制执行与代理推理过程解耦，既保障安全又提升效率。

Result: 相比 OAuth 2.0 Token Exchange (RFC 8693)，CAPMAS 实现了 30 倍的委托操作加速、2 倍的委托延迟降低以及最高 3 倍的带宽节省。其语义范围界定管道在包含超过 3,100 个端点的企业级 API 架构中，能在 17 毫秒内实现超过 90% 的完美权限包检索，并减少了 99.5% 的不必要权限。

Conclusion: CAPMAS 成功在多智能体系统中平衡了安全与效率，通过离线委托和语义级权限界定，在确保严格最小权限原则的同时，大幅降低了延迟与带宽消耗，为实用化的智能体协同执行提供了新的安全架构范式。

Abstract: Agentic systems require secure and efficient delegation of privileges across multiple collaborating agents. Existing approaches fall into two categories. Some propagate user identities directly to agents, obscuring accountability and creating persistent over-privilege risks that are amplified by the non-deterministic behaviour of AI agents. Others rely on continuous synchronization with a central Identity and Access Management (IAM) provider, introducing additional latency and communication overhead.
  We present CAPMAS, a novel architecture for secure end-to-end query execution in multi-agent systems. CAPMAS newly combines a contrastive learning-based semantic scoping pipeline that maps natural-language queries to bounded privilege sets before execution with expressive Macaroon-based tokens that enable offline, tamper-evident delegation with monotonic privilege reduction across agents. By decoupling authentication and delegation enforcement from agent reasoning, CAPMAS enables practical agentic execution while enforcing strict least-privilege guarantees.
  By eliminating synchronous delegation exchanges with the IAM, CAPMAS yields 30 times faster delegation operations, 2 times less delegation-oriented latency and up to 3 times lower bandwidth usage than the OAuth 2.0 Token Exchange (RFC 8693). Its semantic scoping pipeline achieves over 90% perfect privilege-bundle retrieval within 17 milliseconds on enterprise-scale API schemas containing over 3,100 endpoints, while reducing unnecessary privileges by 99.5% when compared to systems that propagate all the user's privileges to agents.

</details>


### [21] [Certifying cooperation: a novel approach to cooperative multi-agent task generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06586)
*Yannick Molinghen,Hugo Charels,Tom Lenaerts*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本论文研究了在共享奖励设置下多智能体何时如何合作的问题。通过设计一个激光学习环境，将合作表示为时间合作图，并定义了六种合作模式。作者使用命题逻辑将环境动态和合作模式编码为查询，用于筛选随机布局生成器以产生具有认证合作需求的任务。通过对五种强化学习算法的实验证明，训练多样性在存在无需合作的解决方案时可提高未见任务的联合成功率；当合作不可或缺时，多样性虽能改善个体退出率，但联合成功率仍接近于零。实证结果揭示了被奖励的部分完成与实际合作之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管共享奖励能为智能体提供共同目标，但它并没有明确规定它们何时、如何甚至是否必须合作才能成功。本研究旨在解答这些基本问题，特别是探索多智能体在路径规划任务中如何形成合作行为。

Method: 建立了激光学习环境（Laser Learning Environment），将合作建模为时间合作图，其中时限边表示帮助者对受益者的支持。定义了六种重叠的图谓词作为合作模式。通过将环境动态和模式谓词编码为命题逻辑公式，区分了某些任务仅在某个获胜轨迹中需要合作，而另一些任务则要求在所有获胜轨迹中都表现出合作。通过将这些查询用作过滤器，可将随机布局采样器转化为生成具有认证合作需求的任务。针对五种多智能体强化学习算法进行了实验，考察了训练多样性对任务表现的影响。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 当任务存在无需合作的解决方案时，训练多样性能显著提高在 unseen 任务上的联合成功率；(2) 当任务强制要求合作时，多样性主要改善了个体智能体的退出率，但联合成功率仍接近于零；(3) 在五个经过认证的合作模式池中，各算法的最终退出率平均值可划分为四个统计上显著不同的水平，但这种排序主要反映了部分完成状况：策略能收集到个体退出的奖励，却很少表现出实现联合成功所需的合作模式。

Conclusion: 该框架通过认证成功的合作需求并利用时间合作图揭示了策略的实际表现，明确揭示了在共享奖励下被奖励的部分完成与实际合作之间的差距。这一发现指出了未来的研究方向，需要开发新的方法以促进真正的合作行为，实现全面的多智能体协作。

Abstract: A shared reward gives agents a common objective, but leaves open when, how and even whether they must cooperate to succeed. We address these questions in the Laser Learning Environment, a multi-agent path-finding environment where cooperation materializes as one agent blocking a laser to let a teammate pass safely. We represent these interactions through temporal cooperation graphs whose timed edges connect helpers to beneficiaries, define six cooperation profiles as overlapping graph predicates, and prove that every cooperative trajectory satisfies at least one. By encoding the environment dynamics and profile predicates as propositional formulae, we distinguish tasks that admit}a profile in some winning trajectory from those that require it in every winning trajectory within a specified horizon. Used as filters, these queries turn a random layout sampler into a generator of tasks with certified cooperation requirements. Experiments with five multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithms show that training diversity improves joint success on unseen tasks when cooperation-free solutions exist. When cooperation is required, greater diversity improves individual-agent exits, but joint success remains near zero. Across five profile-certified pools, final exit rates averaged over algorithms separate the pools into four statistically distinguishable levels but this ordering primarily reflects partial completion: policies collect rewards for individual exits but rarely exhibit the profile required for joint success. Our framework exposes this gap between rewarded partial completion and realized cooperation by certifying what cooperation successful completion requires and using temporal cooperation graphs to reveal what policies exhibit.

</details>


### [22] [Audit Without Verification: When LLM Accountability Layers Relay Rather Than Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07680)
*Paul-Peter Arslan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现多代理LLM管道中的集体责任框架并不能有效降低链长带来的升级风险。删除代理自身结论字段后，准确率提升至45.2%，但遵从性下降至3.4%，且在某些情况下反而损害了准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在跨组织边界的多代理LLM系统中，当故障发生时，需要确定故障进入点。传统的报告机制可能存在信息不完整和误报问题，影响责任归属和故障追踪。

Method: 使用预注册的、分区的六代理管道（两模型，每条链路345,600请求），包含清洁双胞胎（无缺陷）和带有故障注入的数据集。实验包括评估代理报告中的结论与观察，以及删除特定字段对系统性能的影响。

Result: 原假设（集体责任框架能降低链长带来的升级）未被支持。删除代理自身结论字段后，准确率提升41.2个百分点（95%CI +35.3到+46.9），但遵从性从94.4%降至3.4%。当没有任何代理提出正确起源时，审计员仅恢复真实来源的4.1%（低于随机猜测的20%和最佳固定链接审计员的31.0%）。原始文档可达到60.3%的恢复率。

Conclusion: 责任层需要独立于其验证结论的证据。代理自身结论字段的删除揭示了上游可靠性和条件效应的重要性，表明当前的多代理系统在责任归属方面存在严重缺陷。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines increasingly span organisational boundaries; when a fault surfaces, someone must determine where it entered. The artifact available is rarely a full execution trace: it is the reports each agent filed, and a filed report can state a conclusion alongside its observations. Using a pre-registered, institutionally partitioned pipeline of six agents with process-level information boundaries, balanced defect injection and matched clean twins (345,600 requests per chain model, two models), we first report that our pre-registered hypothesis -- that collective responsibility framing degrades escalation with chain length -- is not supported.
  The layer nevertheless fails asymmetrically. It originates almost nothing: zero allegations across 7,996 clean episodes where every agent stayed silent. It filters upstream error poorly, naming an innocent party in 34.4% and 62.6% of clean episodes where an agent raised a false alarm. Conditional on no agent proposing the true origin (59.5% of episodes on one chain model), an auditor reading the reports recovers it in 4.1% of cases -- below a uniform guess (20%) and the best fixed-link accuser (31.0%) -- while reaching 60.3% from the raw documentation of the same episodes.
  Deleting one clause, the field carrying the agents' own conclusion, isolates the cause at constant observations: accuracy rises to 45.2% (+41.2 pp, 95% CI +35.3 to +46.9) and adherence collapses from 94.4% to 3.4%; where the suggestion was correct the same deletion instead costs accuracy, 70.5% to 55.7%. The harm replicates on two frontier auditors in four conditions out of four (+8.5 to +39.0 pp) and in a second domain (+47.7 and +61.1 pp), where the cost disappears. The net effect is governed by upstream reliability together with both conditional magnitudes. An accountability layer needs evidence sufficiently independent of the conclusions it verifies.

</details>


### [23] [Scaling Multi-Agent Systems with Prospect-State Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08033)
*Zhimei Chen,Mu Chen,Fakhri Karray*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了Prospect-State Propagation for Multi-Agent Systems (PspMAS)，一种基于前景理论的多代理系统方法，旨在解决大规模LLM多代理系统中丢失关键经济状态的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM多代理系统在扩展规模时面临挑战，大规模模拟通常会丢弃具有丰富语义的经济状态（即代理行为轨迹），这些状态是宏观经济波动的关键驱动因素。传统的压缩策略导致代理异质性逐渐降低，影响系统表现。

Method: PspMAS将每个代理的微观状态解耦为两个组件：1) 紧凑的前景状态记录心理痕迹，通过轻量级并行化传播器持续注入异质性；2) 利用LLM强大的感知、推理、规划和决策能力构建表达性语义状态。两者协同工作，实现可扩展的LLM多代理仿真方案。

Result: 该方法通过分离前景状态和语义状态，能够有效保留代理行为轨迹等关键信息，同时利用LLM的强大能力提升系统表现，为大规模多代理系统提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Conclusion: PspMAS通过引入前景理论框架和双状态架构，有效解决了大规模多代理系统中状态丢失问题，为复杂系统仿真提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Current LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) periodically compress intermediate states to reduce inference-time token consumption, thereby attempting to incorporate more agents. However, naive scaling strategies face challenges. For example, in economic simulations, large-scale MAS typically discard semantically rich economic states, i.e., agent behavioral trajectories, which are key drivers of macroeconomic fluctuations. In this paper, we reveal a phenomenon in which agent heterogeneity gradually decreases during simulation, and propose Prospect-State Propagation for Multi-Agent Systems (PspMAS). Inspired by prospect theory, PspMAS decouples each agent's micro state into a compact Prospect State and an expressive Semantic State. The former records psychological traces through a lightweight, parallelizable propagator and continuously injects heterogeneity into the system. The latter leverages the strong perception, reasoning, planning, and decision-making abilities of LLMs. These two components work complementarily, providing a scalable LLM-based multi-agent simulation solution.

</details>


### [24] [Rank Without an Oracle: Deviation-Aware Interaction-Rank Selection from Offline Multi-Agent Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08358)
*Xiangwu Wang,Chengwei Cao,Hongyuan Tang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新颖的验证方法 SIRV，用于在日志数据分布与部署/突变分布不匹配时进行多代理博弈模型的选择。它通过分层建模和校准划分来确保模型选择既具备可证明的风险界限，又能保持策略鲁棒性。通过实验证实，SIRV 比基准方法更能降低粗互补均衡（CCE）差距，并提供了更精确的可证明收益，这凸显了可证明的模型选择与普适性策略改进之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线多代理代理评估基于日志数据进行训练，但模型将在不同的部署分布下使用。这可能导致标准验证方法更倾向于预测日志表现而非真实的战略激励，从而无法选择出真正鲁棒的策略。研究需要一种能在有限游戏域内提供有限风险界限和 CCE 差距证书的新验证框架，同时在模型选择时避免过度拟合日志数据。

Method: 我们引入了**选择性交互排名验证 (SIRV)** 技术：(1) 在训练划分中，使用嵌套代理对多种候选的部署和单边突变分布进行建模；(2) 构建这些分布的通用联合体；(3) 在独立的校准划分上对每个候选进行评估，确保评估使用相同的联合分布。(4) SIRV 选择满足同时上界风险界限与最佳分数之间的最小排名；如果目标不受支持或估计不精确，则拒绝。(5) 推广出的共同覆盖事件提供了有限候选的风险界限和特定候选的 CCE 差距证书。(6) 此外，作者还证明了一个关于离散支持点非可识别性的精确结果，并在分析中使用经验-伯恩斯坦界限来增强保底规则。

Result: 大规模实验（2,048 个游戏/家族）表明：使用经验-伯恩斯坦界限，可证明的 CCE 差距中位数比传统的 Hoeffding 界限降低了 42.5%，支持的回报平均提高了 1.36 个单位。在另一种拥堵游戏族群中，SIRV-EB 保底规则在选择真实策略时显著降低了 CCE 遗憾，同时保持了游戏级损失。175 个游戏中的实证分析显示，当覆盖不充分时，ID-Mean 方法的 CCE 遗憾仍为正值，但可证明的回报大幅下降。上述发现表明，可证明的模型选择（SIRV）与其声称的普适性策略改进是两个独立的步骤。

Conclusion: SIRV 提供了一种系统性的方法，可以在离线日志环境中进行可靠的策略选择，产生可验证的风险界限和 CCE 差距证书。它不仅改善了实证性能，还将可证明的模型验证与普适性游戏改进区分开来，为有限博弈中的离线强化学习指明了新的方向。

Abstract: Offline multi-agent payoff models are estimated under a logging distribution but used on distributions induced by learned solutions and unilateral deviations. Standard held-out loss can therefore favor an interaction class that predicts logged play well while distorting strategic incentives. We introduce Selective Interaction-Rank Validation (SIRV) for finite games with known logging distributions. A training split fits nested payoff models and constructs a common union of all candidate deployment and unilateral-replacement distributions; an independent calibration split evaluates every candidate on this same union. SIRV returns the smallest rank whose simultaneous upper worst-target risk is within tolerance of the best upper score, and abstains when a declared target is unsupported or too imprecisely estimated. A common coverage event yields a finite-candidate target-risk bound and a candidate-specific coarse correlated equilibrium (CCE) gap certificate. We also isolate an exact two-point off-support non-identifiability result. In a controlled factorial study with 2,048 independent games per family, empirical-Bernstein bounds reduce the median CCE-gap certificate by 42.5% relative to Hoeffding bounds on common returns, with a 1.36-point reduction in supported return. Under paired rank misspecification and in a separately generated congestion family, the SIRV-EB fallback rule lowers mean true candidate-selection CCE regret relative to ID-Mean, while retaining game-level losses. Across 384 games at $N=3,5,8$, ID-Mean-relative mean CCE-regret effects stay positive while certified return falls sharply under weak coverage. These results separate certifiable model selection from universal strategic improvement.

</details>


### [25] [Reachability-Certified Subteam Decomposition for Locally Interacting Multi-Agent MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08366)
*Xiangwu Wang,Chengwei Cao,Hongyuan Tang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出 RCSD，一种面向多智能体有限 MDP 的可达性认证子团队分解方法，用当前状态下的可达接触时间和奖励上界来构造智能体间亲和度，从而在通信受限条件下指导持久化团队划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于距离或当前接触的子团队划分不足：相距较远的智能体未来可能交互，而高奖励也可能因折扣或不可达性而难以兑现。通信容量限制要求系统提前决定哪些智能体可协调，因此需要更可靠的划分依据。

Method: RCSD结合成对接触时间的速度下界与奖励包络，形成当前状态亲和度；对任意满足容量约束的持久划分，用割亲和度之和界定奖励删除误差；再基于割 MDP 的团队最优策略构造集中最优策略的近似。

Result: 理论上有最坏情况紧的误差与 regret 上界，其中割 MDP 团队策略乘积相对集中最优的 regret 至多为该证书的两倍。实验显示，在受控五智能体任务中，RCSD-Exact 相比均匀、仅距离、仅奖励包络划分显著降低归一化执行 regret；大规模实验中，RCSD 在部分场景优于距离和当前接触分组，但在更大智能体集合上表现混合，且构造时间在中位数上低于 1 秒。

Conclusion: RCSD 的核心价值在于把可达性与奖励可达性结合进子团队分解，为通信受限多智能体系统提供可验证的近似保证。它适合需要持久团队划分和理论误差界定的场景，但在更大、更复杂系统中仍需结合控制器库、规划成本和任务分布进一步优化。

Abstract: Persistent communication limits force a multi-agent system to decide which agents may coordinate throughout a rollout. Current proximity alone is insufficient: separated agents may interact later, whereas a large pair reward may remain unreachable until it is heavily discounted. We introduce Reachability-Certified Subteam Decomposition (RCSD) for finite multi-agent Markov decision processes with factorized physical dynamics, finite-range ordered pair rewards, and almost-sure motion bounds. RCSD combines a speed-limit lower bound on pairwise contact time with a reward envelope to form a current-state affinity. For any capacity-valid persistent partition, the sum of cut affinities bounds the reward-deletion error of every unchanged stationary Markov state-feedback policy. A product of team-optimal policies for the resulting cut MDP incurs at most twice this certificate in regret against the centralized optimum. Both bounds are worst-case tight. On a controlled five-agent family, RCSD-Exact reduces aggregate normalized execution regret by 56.0%, 28.8%, and 25.3% relative to uniform, distance-only, and envelope-only partitions. A separate stochastic two-dimensional study finds no bound violation over 384 exact-partition and 1,440 restricted-controller evaluations. Exact four-agent evidence favors RCSD over uniform and distance-only grouping; raw evidence for current contact is borderline and envelope-only is unresolved. Across balanced 8-20-agent strata, controller-library utility is mixed: pointwise paired intervals favor RCSD over distance and current contact, include zero for uniform, and favor envelope-only and Value-MIP over RCSD. Partition construction remains subsecond in median up to 100 agents; this last result does not include affinity formation or MDP planning.

</details>


### [26] [Beyond Agent Harnesses: Cross-Substrate Authority for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08472)
*Yang Li,Sergey Volkov,Hai Liu,Zongsi Xu,Xiyu Chen,Tuo Zhou,Dian Shao,Hao Sun,Ye Lu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文研究了代理系统中的跨底层权限差距，即授权信息位于规划器不可见的工作区或记忆状态之外。通过三个实验对比规划观察增强与执行时权限检查，发现执行边界的权限强制执行是记忆治理的操作终点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理系统在持久化模型可见记忆的同时变更工作区，而运行时、注册表或审批服务可能在两者之外持有权威状态，导致相同的最终文件可能需要相反的安全操作。这种跨底层权限差距使得决策相关的授权信息无法被规划器感知，亟需解决。

Method: 在两个受控的小型基准测试族上进行三个实验：实验1为128格受控证据消融实验，对比无权限候选证据、原始凭证和类型化关系；实验2使用96次规划调用，评估工作区可见证据和类型化关系的规划可靠性；实验3重放32个固定的模型生成首动作意图，零额外模型调用，使用确定性执行防护。均采用真实Git谱系、持久记录的代理执行尝试、确定性预言机和两条模型路由。

Result: 实验1：无权限证据0/32语义成功，原始凭证和类型化关系均32/32成功，缺失的权限事实解释了提升，类型化封装未带来额外规划准确性增益。实验2：工作区可见证据导致12/16不安全发布决策，类型化关系规划仍不可靠（15/32首动作正确，11/32无效或缺失）。实验3：确定性执行防护阻止所有6个不安全意图生效，允许所有12个有效授权发布意图。

Conclusion: 结果将权限强制执行定位在变更边界，作为记忆治理的操作终点。

Abstract: Agentic systems persist model-visible memory while mutating workspaces, while a runtime, registry, or approval service may hold authority state outside both. Identical final files can then require opposite safe actions. We call this the cross-substrate authority gap: decision- relevant authorization information resides outside the planner-visible workspace or memory state. Across two controlled mini-benchmark families, three experiments compare planner-observation augmentation with an execution-time authority check using real Git lineage, durably recorded agent execution attempts, deterministic oracles, and two model routes. Experiment 1 is a 128-cell controlled evidence ablation: authority-blind candidate evidence obtains 0/32 final semantic success, while raw receipts and a typed relation both obtain 32/32. The missing authority fact accounts for the gain; typed packaging provides no observed planning-accuracy gain over equal raw information. Experiment 2 uses 96 planning calls: workspace-visible evidence yields 12/16 unsafe publication decisions, and planning with the typed relation remains unreliable (15/32 first actions correct; 11/32 invalid or absent). Experiment 3 replays the same 32 fixed model-generated first-action intents with zero additional model calls; a deterministic execution guard prevents all six unsafe intents from becoming effects and permits all 12 valid authorized publish intents. These results position authority enforcement at the mutation boundary as the operational endpoint of memory governance.

</details>


### [27] [MorphoOrgaAgent: A Foundation-Model-Based Multi-Agent System for Autonomous Organoid Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08696)
*Hanyi Zhang,Maximilian Hoermann,Lion J. Gleiter,Yiling Xu,Bettina Katalin Budai,Hans-Ulrich Kauczor,Carsten Marr,Tingying Peng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出 MorphoOrgaAgent，一个面向类器官形态分析的多智能体框架，可实现零样本类器官分割、自动统计分析、可视化与报告生成，并配套发布 MorphoOrgaVQA 基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类器官形态特征提取依赖人工分割，耗时且劳动强度大；后续定量统计还需要编程和数学背景，限制了实验生物学家使用。

Method: 框架包含三个核心模块：TaskUnderstandingAgent 负责理解用户自然语言需求并确定测量指标和可视化类型；混合分割模块结合 Cellpose 几何提示和文本提示引导 SAM3 进行零样本实例分割；ReportAgent 计算定量指标并生成结构化分析报告。此外，论文提出 MorphoOrgaVQA 基准用于评估智能体系统在类器官形态分析中的表现。

Result: 实验表明，MorphoOrgaAgent 能处理明确指令和描述性请求，生成与真实标注接近的测量结果，并在无需手工编程的情况下输出完整分析报告。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了类器官形态定量分析的门槛，将分割、统计和报告流程整合为自然语言驱动的工作流，具有较好的自动化和可解释性，适合推动实验生物学家更便捷地开展形态学研究。

Abstract: Organoids are three-dimensional tissue models whose morphology provides important insights into tumor development, disease progression, and drug testing. Extracting these morphological features relies heavily on manual segmentation, which is time-consuming and labor-intensive. Furthermore, performing quantitative statistical analysis typically requires custom coding skills and a mathematical background, presenting a major barrier for experimental biologists. To address these challenges, we introduce MorphoOrgaAgent, a multi-agent framework that achieves zero-shot organoid segmentation, automated data analysis, and report generation based on natural language input. The framework consists mainly of three core components: a TaskUnderstandingAgent that identifies requested measurements and visualization types; a hybrid segmentation module that combines Cellpose-derived geometric prompts with text prompts to guide SAM3 for zero-shot organoid instance segmentation; and a ReportAgent that computes quantitative metrics and compiles them alongside generated visualizations into a structured report. We further introduce MorphoOrgaVQA, a benchmark designed for quantitative evaluation of agent systems in organoid morphology analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that MorphoOrgaAgent handles both explicit and descriptive user requests, produces measurements closely matching ground truth, and generates complete analysis reports without requiring manual programming. The complete source code and MorphoOrgaVQA benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/peng-lab/MorphoOrgaAgent.

</details>


### [28] [Copying explains the collective behavior of AI agents in the wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09150)
*Giordano De Marzo,Nicola Alboré,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究发现，AI代理在没有任务指令的情况下，仅凭对当前可见编辑内容的概率复制，就能在公开维基上产生复杂的集体行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨无外部协作指令下，代理如何通过局部信息自发形成惯例和结构，以理解简单复制机制在群体行为中的作用。

Method: 利用代理可观察的编辑历史（当前页面、最近编辑流及较旧内容），为三个决策（写作位置、自我命名、信息措辞）分别建立一个仅含一个自由参数的概率复制模型；通过拟合模型预测与实际数据中的重尾分布、名称片段频率及页面内部一致性特征来验证模型。

Result: 三个简易复制模型成功再现了代理在单页聚集人数的重尾分布、名称构成片段的使用频率以及页面间既内部一致又互不相同的拼贴格局；结果表明早期编辑者的选择会对后来者产生强烈的惯例引导效应。

Conclusion: 仅凭代理对环境中可见选项份额的概率复制，即可生成群体的主要集体结构，且这种机制使群体极易被先行者或安静时期的编辑者所塑造成规范。

Abstract: In June 2026, thousands of AI agents found that a small public wiki would accept edits from inside their sandboxes, and started using it to help one another pass a timed test. Each agent lived for about an hour and remembered nothing afterwards. Nobody asked them to cooperate, and the wiki had not been built for them. The complete record of what they wrote is public, and it is unusually informative, because it preserves not only what each agent wrote but what that agent could see before writing. We use it to follow the three decisions an agent had to make on arrival: where to write, what to call itself, and how to word its message. One rule governs all three. An agent takes an option with a probability close to the share of that option in what it can see, and the share that matters is the one on the page in front of it, then the one in the stream of recent edits, and only weakly anything older. Three minimal copying models, one per decision and with a single free parameter each, reproduce the heavy-tailed distribution of how many agents met on a page, the frequency of the pieces from which the agents built their names, and the patchwork of pages that are internally consistent and different from one another. Copying whatever the environment happens to show is enough to produce most of the collective structure of this population. It is also what makes such a population easy to steer, since whoever writes first, or writes while the others are quiet, sets the convention for everyone who comes later.

</details>

### [29] [Benchmarking Storage Systems for Machine Learning Workloads Using NIO Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05418)
*Jonathan W. Morris,Ionut Mistreanu,Connor Louie*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: NIO Bench 基准测试框架分析六类 ML 模型存储 I/O 模式，发现 I/O 集中于数据准备、模型加载和检查点，训练为计算密集型，存储访问遵循幂律分布，读取尾延迟为主要瓶颈，建议优化预取、页缓存固定和突发检查点写入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多关注计算吞吐，忽视文件系统 I/O 行为，ML 训练对存储有独特需求，需要专门的存储 I/O 特征分析框架。

Method: 提出 NIO Bench 框架，采用双层跟踪：Python 级 I/O 挂钩提供语义阶段上下文，Linux strace 覆盖完整系统调用包括 DataLoader 子进程；在 Nautilus Kubernetes 集群上部署 Ceph 分布式文件系统评估六种模型架构。

Result: I/O 集中在数据准备、模型加载、检查点阶段；数据暂存后训练为计算密集型；存储访问呈极端幂律：<10% 文件占 >90% 字节传输；分布式存储缓存未命中导致的读取尾延迟是主要存储瓶颈。

Conclusion: 针对 ML 优化的存储系统应优先考虑激进数据预取、页缓存固定和高效处理突发检查点写入。

Abstract: Machine learning training workloads place unique demands on storage systems, yet most existing benchmarks focus on computational throughput rather than file system I/O behavior. We present a benchmarking framework, Neural I/O Benchmark (NIO Bench), that characterizes storage access patterns across six diverse ML model architectures: Language Transformers, Vision Transformers, Diffusion Models, Spiking Neural Networks, Artificial Neural Networks, and Reinforcement Learning. Our framework employs a two-layer tracing approach combining Python-level I/O hooks for semantic phase context with Linux strace for complete syscall coverage including DataLoader worker subprocesses. We evaluate all six models on a Nautilus Kubernetes cluster with Ceph distributed file system. Our results reveal that I/O is heavily concentrated in data preparation, model loading, and model checkpointing. We also found that training is compute-bound rather than data-bound once data is staged, and that storage access follows an extreme power law where fewer than 10% of files account for over 90% of bytes transferred, and that read tail latency from cache misses on distributed storage is the primary storage bottleneck. These findings suggest that storage systems optimized for ML should prioritize aggressive data prefetching, page cache pinning, and efficient handling of bursty checkpoint writes.

</details>

Conclusion: 共应RAGMark为RAG系统应于世界的源源，常览于RAG组仰，完整生流，电功，内容减缩，个RAG维结系统（RAGMark），允许使用者显老RAG部件中的不同完成调整，为大型RAG系统的接口，完整生流，电功，）的接口，，公开源代码（https://github.com/zferic/RAGMark）为完成下私的程式。

Abstract: We present RAGMark, a modular benchmarking framework for advanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems targeting small-scale multi-GPU environments. RAGMark evaluates diverse RAG components, including retrievers, vector databases, prompt-processing methods, and generator models, while collecting detailed per-stage metrics such as latency, GPU utilization, memory consumption, power usage, time to first token (TTFT), throughput, and answer quality. The framework is highly extensible, separating RAG stages, timing, and resource monitoring into modular components, and is designed to efficiently sweep large configuration spaces while minimizing repeated model and database initialization overhead. Using RAGMark, we characterize five RAG workloads on open-domain QA datasets across varying retrieval depths, model scales, reranking, compression methods, and vector database configurations. We show that while autoregressive generation dominates latency in naive pipelines, context-reduction techniques shift bottlenecks across compute, memory bandwidth, and preprocessing stages. Reranking and compression produce compounding benefits: reranking reduces compression workload itself, while both jointly reduce prefill and KV-cache traversal costs, lowering energy consumption by up to 66%. We further observe strong cross-stage interactions, where small upstream context reductions cascade through downstream latency, memory traffic, and energy consumption. The RAGMark source code is publicly available at: https://github.com/zferic/RAGMark.

</details>


### [31] [RGB Input Pipelines: Throughput, GPU Memory, and Transformation Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06635)
*Vladimir Iglovikov*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 论文比较了七条图像增强输入路径从RGB JPEG到同步CUDA float16批次的性能，结果显示DALI在共享配方上整体吞吐更高，而AlbumentationsX在更多单库配方覆盖比较中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像增强流水线必须在模型训练前完成整批数据处理；不同库在吞吐和GPU显存占用上差异明显，因此需要可控、可复现的端到端输入准备基准比较。

Method: 研究从57个精选增强配方、批量大小256、NVIDIA L4 GPU出发，人工对齐多库变换配方和参数；记录759次测量中的吞吐量与峰值进程GPU显存，并对共享配方进行配对比较，同时统计AlbumentationsX RGB目录覆盖情况。

Result: 在11个所有路径共享的配方上，DALI与AlbumentationsX的中位吞吐量分别为5029和4679 images/s，峰值GPU显存分别为2086和1852 MiB。配对比较中，AlbumentationsX在TorchVision、Kornia、Pillow的多数配方中胜出；但DALI在全部22个共享配方上快于AlbumentationsX，中位吞吐比为1.18倍。

Conclusion: 该研究表明DALI更适合追求高吞吐的同步GPU输入管线，而AlbumentationsX在增强覆盖和多数单库配方性能上表现突出；但结论仅适用于固定设置下的输入准备，不代表训练速度、数值等价性或各库最优调参结果。

Abstract: An image-augmentation pipeline must deliver a complete batch before a model can use it. We compare seven input paths from five libraries, starting with RGB JPEG files and ending with a synchronized CUDA float16 batch. We manually matched transformation recipes and parameters across libraries to make the workloads as comparable as possible. The experiment uses 57 selected recipes, a batch size of 256, and one NVIDIA L4 machine. Throughput and peak process GPU memory are recorded together in 759 measurements. On the 11 recipes shared by all paths, DALI and AlbumentationsX have median throughputs of 5,029 and 4,679 images/s, with median peak GPU memory of 2,086 and 1,852 MiB. Broader pairwise comparisons favor AlbumentationsX on 26/26 TorchVision recipes, 50/51 Kornia recipes, and 25/26 Pillow recipes. DALI is faster than AlbumentationsX on all 22 shared recipes, with a median throughput ratio of 1.18x. A separate census reports coverage of the 118 entries in a selected AlbumentationsX RGB catalog. The study measures input preparation at fixed settings; it does not measure model training, numerical equivalence, or the best attainable configuration of each library. Benchmark code: https://github.com/albumentations-team/benchmark.

</details>


### [32] [Mathematical Modeling of a Cognitive Continuum Digital Shadow for Large-Scale, Cross-Facility Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07275)
*Mark Asch,Marius Garénaux Gruau,François Bodin*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: The paper introduces the Cognitive Continuum Digital Shadow (CCDS), a decision-support layer for exascale scientific workflows. It uses state-space representation and multistage stochastic programming to optimize deployment scenarios before job launch, quantifying cost, makespan, and energy trade-offs under uncertainty. The optimization is modeled as a multimode, resource-constrained, stochastic supply-chain network design problem and demonstrated on a genomics workflow.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To help operators and users quantify cost, makespan, and energy trade-offs of scientific workflows under uncertain resource availability, enabling better decision-making before job submission.

Method: Formulates the problem as a multimode, resource-constrained, stochastic supply-chain network design problem using a state-space representation of the continuum coupled with multistage stochastic programming.

Result: A mathematical framework for CCDS is presented, demonstrated on a realistic genomics workflow scheduled across heterogeneous HPC and data-center resources, showing its ability to explore and optimize deployment scenarios.

Conclusion: This paper establishes the mathematical foundations for CCDS, with subsequent papers covering software architecture and large-scale use-cases.

Abstract: We present the mathematical foundations of a \emph{Cognitive Continuum Digital Shadow} (CCDS), a decision-support layer between users and the cross-facility infrastructure---instruments, networks, data stores and compute centers---of exascale and post-exascale scientific workflows. The CCDS couples a state-space representation of the continuum with multistage stochastic programming, so that deployment scenarios can be explored and optimized \emph{before} jobs are launched. This allows operators and users to quantify the cost, makespan and energy trade-offs of a workflow under uncertain resource availability, and hedge their decisions accordingly. We formulate the underlying optimization as a multimode, resource-constrained, stochastic supply-chain network design problem and demonstrate it on a realistic genomics workflow scheduled across heterogeneous HPC and data-center resources. This is the first of three papers; the second treats the underlying software architecture and the third reports large-scale use-cases.

</details>

### [33] [WIP: Energy-Efficient LLM-Based Serving Cluster Formulation in Cell-Free Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05725)
*Marcin Hoffmann,Paweł Kryszkiewicz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI代理方法，用于解决6G用户中心无小区大规模多输入多输出（UCCF MMMIMO）网络中的服务集群构建（SCF）问题，以提升能源效率（EE）。实验表明，该方法相比基线实现了高达32%的能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高6G无线网络的能源效率（EE）需要更高效地利用现有基础设施。在UCCF MMMIMO中，关键挑战在于服务集群构建（SCF），即如何决策哪些基站同时为单个用户提供服务，而传统算法在性能与复杂度之间难以取得最佳平衡。

Method: 提出采用基于大语言模型（LLM）的人工智能（AI）代理来处理SCF问题。利用复杂的、基于三维射线追踪的蜂窝网络模拟器进行评估，并对比了多种GPT模型与先进算法。

Result: 评估结果表明，所提出的AI代理在能源效率（EE）方面表现优异，相比基线方案实现了高达32%的增益。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的AI代理能够有效解决6G UCCF网络中的服务集群构建问题，显著提升能源效率，为构建高效、绿色的下一代无线网络提供了可行方案。

Abstract: One way to increase the Energy Efficiency (EE) of 6G wireless networks is to utilize existing network infrastructure more efficiently. This can be achieved by introducing User-Centric Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input-Multiple-Output (UCCF MMMIMO), which allows for simultaneously serving a single user by multiple Base Stations (BSs). From this perspective, the key challenge is to decide which BSs should serve a given user, known as the Serving Cluster Formulation (SCF). In this paper, we propose to deal with this problem by using an Artificial Intelligence (AI) agent based on a Large Language Model (LLM), targeting improvement of EE. We evaluated the proposed AI agent using a complex, 3D Ray Tracer-based, cellular network simulator, comparing a few GPT models and state-of-the-art algorithms. The results show up to 32% gain in EE of the proposed AI agent compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [34] [SDN-Orchestrated Dual-Path 5G/SATCOM Maritime Communications for Carrier Strike Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05729)
*Avinash Srinivasan,Dalibor Španjević,Kevin H. Nguyen,Bannon Ireton,Christopher B. Landis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种融合SDN与SDR的框架，统一控制5G和卫星通信，实现航母打击群通信的双路径故障切换，显著提升了海上通信可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航母打击群通信面临异构链路质量随距离、天气、衰落和间歇性中断而变化的挑战，现有单一链路或确定性抽象无法满足任务流量的可靠性需求。

Method: 构建集中式SDN控制器，耦合自适应调制编码的莱斯衰落5G信道、ITU-R雨衰减模型的Ku波段卫星链路及Gilbert-Elliott突发可用性模型，基于业务类别和链路状态进行逐包路由；采用随机仿真，在四个作战区域开展60次独立试验，每次50000个包，使用共同随机数、配对置信区间和收敛诊断进行统计分析。

Result: 在诺福克基准天气下，SDN路由可靠性达93.09%，远高于仅5G（36.22%）和仅卫星（89.87%），配对增益分别为+56.87和+3.22个百分点；在低莱斯K因子/高突发故障压力下优势扩大，AMC随信干噪比恶化改善5G鲁棒性；SDN在四个区域均保持最高可靠性。

Conclusion: 跨层SDN控制的故障切换无需依赖确定性链路抽象，即可显著增强海上通信韧性，为异构军事网络提供了可扩展的弹性解决方案。

Abstract: Carrier Strike Group (CSG) communications must sustain mission traffic across heterogeneous links whose quality varies with distance, weather, fading, and intermittent outages. We present an integrated Software-Defined Networking (SDN)/Software-Defined Radio (SDR) framework that unifies 5G and satellite communications (SATCOM) under a single control plane for dual-path failover in CSG communications. The framework is evaluated via stochastic simulation across four operational regions: Norfolk, the Norwegian Sea, the Philippine Sea, and the North Pacific storm track. The framework couples a centralized SDN controller with a Rician-faded 5G channel under Adaptive Modulation and Coding (AMC), a Ku-band SATCOM channel with ITU-R P.618/P.838 rain attenuation, and Gilbert-Elliott burst-availability processes on both links. The controller routes each packet by traffic class and current link state. Experiments use 60 independent trials of 50,000 packets per parameter setting with common random numbers, paired confidence intervals, and convergence diagnostics across fault, fading, and region sweeps. For the Norfolk weather baseline, SDN routing reaches 93.09% reliability versus 36.22% (5G-only) and 89.87% (SATCOM-only), with paired gains of +56.87 and +3.22 percentage points. The advantage grows under joint low-Rician-K/high-burst-fault stress, and AMC improves 5G robustness as Signal-to-Interference-plus-Noise Ratio degrades. SDN remains the highest-reliability mode across all four regions, indicating that cross-layer SDN-controlled failover improves maritime resilience without relying on deterministic link abstractions.

</details>


### [35] [Semantic Communication for Distributed Spectrum Monitoring over Unreliable Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05791)
*Samer Lahoud,Kinda Khawam*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 分布式频谱监测中的语义通信方法：接收机将时频观测映射为紧凑潜在表示，在不可靠链路末端通过端到端训练实现发射器计数与定位，仅需少量比特即可在变成员环境下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享不可靠报告链路上，原始I/Q样本转发不切实际。需将发射器计数与定位形式化为语义通信问题，以应对报告丢失和成员变化。

Method: 每个接收机局部时频观测编码为潜在表示，融合节点将存活的潜在与接收机位置配对，作为无序集进行融合，支持可变成员数。编解码器在报告丢失和有限率量化下端到端训练。

Result: 实验表明，感知训练提升了抗擦性；集合融合优于固定序拼接；单一模型无需重训即可适应不同接收机数量；每个潜在组件仅需少量比特，率膝点在不同擦除水平下保持稳定。

Conclusion: 低率语义报告在不可靠链路上的分布式频谱监测是可行的，实现了高鲁棒性和低带宽需求。

Abstract: Distributed spectrum monitoring relies on spatially separated receivers to infer the state of the radio environment. A central task is to determine how many emitters are active and where they are located. Sensors typically report over shared unreliable links, where contention, fading, or energy limits erase whole reports, so forwarding raw I/Q samples from all receivers to a fusion node becomes impractical. This paper formulates distributed emitter counting and localization as a semantic communication problem over unreliable reporting links. Each receiver maps its local time-frequency observation to a compact latent representation and transmits it. The fusion node pairs each surviving latent with the corresponding receiver position and operates on the resulting unordered set, which keeps the fusion rule compatible with variable membership and receiver-count changes. The encoder and decoder are trained end-to-end with report erasure, and optionally finite-rate quantization, inside the task objective.
