In this paper, we propose Agent ATO (Agentic Trajectory Observer), a tool for visualizing AI coding agent interaction timelines from console logs. Agent ATO extracts agent interactions, classifies them by command or tool type, and visualizes them as timelines. In addition to an all-interaction timeline, Agent ATO provides filtered timelines that emphasize file discovery, file reading, file editing, and execution while preserving surrounding context. We illustrate how Agent ATO may help developers inspect and compare agent actions using selected runs from two repair tasks. Future work will apply Agent ATO to more agents, tasks, and development environments, and will evaluate whether it reduces the effort needed to compare trajectories.

</details>


### [104] [AttnCompress: Dynamic Attention-Guided Trajectory Compression for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08318)
*Zhengran Zeng,Yixin Li,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于注意力的动态上下文压缩框架AttnCompress，用于解决自主软件工程代理中长交互轨迹导致的上下文窗口限制和成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩方法采用静态剪枝策略，存在粒度不匹配，无法保留语义依赖和语法细节，导致在复杂SE任务中性能受限。

Method: AttnCompress包含三个机制：通过困惑度峰值进行结构感知分段，利用代理注意力权重估计相关性，以及动态滚动窗口重新评估和召回历史上下文。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified和Multi-SWE-Bench上，AttnCompress达到53.17%的通过率，优于先前最优基线，同时减少token消耗21.6%，总成本降低33.6%，且模型无关，跨语言泛化良好。

Conclusion: AttnCompress有效平衡语义完整性与动态适应性，显著降低上下文长度与成本，适用于多种编程语言的自主软件工程任务。

Abstract: The transition from human-centric assistance to Autonomous Software Engineering (ASE) agents has enabled the resolution of complex real-world SE tasks. However, the trial-and-error nature of these agents generates lengthy interaction trajectories, creating severe bottlenecks in terms of context window limits and cost. While context compression offers a potential remedy, prior approaches suffer from static pruning strategies and granularity mismatches, often failing to preserve the semantic dependencies and syntactic details crucial for SE tasks. To strictly preserve critical task evidence while reducing context length, we introduce AttnCompress, a dynamic attention-guided trajectory compression framework. Unlike existing approaches, AttnCompress bridges the gap between semantic integrity and dynamic adaptability through three key mechanisms: (1) structure-aware segmentation via perplexity (PPL) spikes to preserve the syntactic structure of code and logs; (2) relevance estimation using proxy attention weights to quantify the precise relevance of historical blocks to the agent's current reasoning; and (3) a dynamic rolling window to re-evaluate and recall historical context as the task evolves. Extensive evaluation on SWE-Bench-Verified and Multi-SWE-Bench demonstrates that AttnCompress achieves a pass rate of 53.17%, outperforming prior state-of-the-art baselines while reducing token consumption by 21.6% and total costs by 33.6%. The framework proves to be model-agnostic and generalizes effectively across diverse programming languages.

</details>


### [105] [Tool Retrievers Are Underestimated: Annotation Expansion Reveals True Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08327)
*Yanyu Zhu,Chenheng Zhang,Shaoshen Chen,Hoilam Pao,Yufei zhang,Jiajun Chai,Dongnian Wang,Zhaoyu Hu,Guojun Yin,Wei Lin,Hai-Tao Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ToolEX 框架自动发现功能等效的工具组合，将单一标注扩展为多个有效组合，揭示了现有基准因一对一标注而系统低估检索器的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放世界中，同一功能常由多种不同工具实现，导致查询对应的工具组合本质上是一对多，而现有基准仅给出单一标注，使得等效的检索结果被误判为失败。

Method: 提出 ToolEX 框架，通过自动挖掘与已标注工具组合在功能上等效的替代组合，对 Tool-DE 基准的 7,360 条子查询进行扩展，得到平均每查询 5.3 种有效组合的 ToolEQ 基准；随后在 ToolEQ 上重新评估八种基线检索器和两种微调变体。

Result: 在 ToolEQ 上的检索指标显著高于 Tool-DE，表明一对一标注导致对检索器的系统性低估；微调带来的提升中有 30%--47% 为评估伪象，并非真实改进。将同一流程应用于 SkillRet 技能检索同样验证了一对一问题的普遍性。

Conclusion: 现有工具（技能）检索基准的单一标注掩盖了功能等效解的丰富性，导致评估偏低；采用等效扩展的基准能更真实地反映检索器能力，微调的实际收益需在此更全面的评估下重新衡量。

Abstract: In open-world scenarios with massive and evolving tool repositories, tool-augmented large language models rely on a retriever to surface relevant tools for a given query. Because such repositories often contain many tools that implement the same functionality, a single query can often be resolved by several distinct but functionally equivalent tool combinations, making the natural query-to-tool mapping inherently one-to-many. However, existing tool retrieval benchmarks annotate each query with a single relevant tool combination, collapsing this one-to-many mapping into a rigid one-to-one annotation and causing valid retrieved tools to be misjudged as failures. To address this, we propose ToolEX (Tool Equivalent eXpansion), a framework that automatically discovers and annotates the tool combinations functionally equivalent to the labeled ones. Applied to the 7,360-query Tool-DE benchmark, ToolEX finds that 67.9% of sub-queries admit equivalent alternatives, expanding the singular ground truth to an average of 5.3 valid combinations per query. Using the expanded benchmark ToolEQ, we re-evaluate eight base retrievers and two fine-tuned variants; metrics on ToolEQ rise substantially over Tool-DE, showing that one-to-one annotation systematically underestimates retrievers and that 30--47% of the reported fine-tuning gain is an evaluation artifact rather than genuine improvement. Applying the same pipeline to skill retrieval on SkillRet further confirms that the one-to-one problem extends beyond tool retrieval.

</details>


### [106] [RepoNav: From Snippet Retrieval to File-Centered Repository Navigation for Code Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08355)
*Hongzheng Chai,Jiakun Li,Hongyue Yu,Yuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoNav 是一种轻量级的检索后接口，通过将检索到的代码片段重新组织为以文件为中心的导航脚手架，提供紧凑的结构线索和候选目标，帮助 LLM 代理对比同级相似符号，从而显著提升函数级定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代码检索工具通常返回扁平、孤立的代码片段列表，虽能提示相关文件，但无法提供足够的结构化信息，导致 LLM 代理难以在同一个文件内语义相似的多个候选函数中精准区分并选择真正的目标函数。

Method: 引入 RepoNav，作为一个轻量级的后检索接口。它将检索到的 snippets 重新组织为以文件为中心的导航脚手架，展示紧凑的结构线索和候选目标，引导代理进行按需的文件结构浏览，并在选择目标前对同级符号进行对比分析。

Result: 在 LocBench 上对多种模型进行的实验表明，RepoNav 显著提高了函数级定位性能，并缩小了文件到函数的差距。消融实验证实，性能提升源于结构化的证据组织，而非仅仅暴露额外的文件结构。此外，该方法还在仓库级问答基准上带来了性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的证据组织（如以文件为中心的导航脚手架）来引导 LLM 代理进行符号对比和按需导航，是提高仓库级代码任务中函数定位准确性的有效途径，能有效弥合文件检索与精确函数定位之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Solving repository-level code tasks requires LLM-based agents to use code search tools to navigate large codebases and identify a small set of relevant files and functions. However, current retrieval tools typically return flat lists of isolated code snippets: such lists can surface relevant files, but provide insufficient structure for agents to distinguish the target function from semantically similar alternatives in the same file. We introduce RepoNav, a lightweight post-retrieval interface that reorganizes retrieved snippets into a file-centered navigation scaffold. By presenting compact structural cues and candidate targets, this scaffold guides on-demand file-structure browsing, helping agents compare sibling symbols before selecting a target function. Across diverse models on LocBench, RepoNav improves function-level localization and narrows the file-to-function gap. Controlled ablations demonstrate that these gains come from structured evidence organization rather than simply exposing additional file structure, and the approach also improves performance on a repository-level question-answering benchmark.

</details>


### [107] [Authority Is Not a String: A Capability-Scoped Harness for Prompt-Injection-Resistant Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08371)
*Dimitrios Stamatios Bouras,Yihan Dai,Sergey Mechtaev*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 CapScope，一种面向编码代理的细粒度授权机制，通过限制工具调用权限来缓解间接提示注入问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理通常可使用沙箱内的高权限工具读取文件、执行命令和修改代码。由于只需知道资源名称即可操作，恶意文本或工具输出中的注入指令可能诱导代理执行用户未授权的行为。

Method: CapScope 在 harness 层根据可信输入预先推导任务级权限上限，并为不同子代理分配彼此隔离的类型化能力。这些能力不进入模型上下文，所有工具调用都会在执行前按调用者代理的能力进行检查。

Result: 实验在 Pi 编码代理的修复流程中进行，覆盖 5 个 Python 任务、5 种注入面、4 种授权条件和 300 次运行。相比环境级权限和全局策略基线，CapScope 下注入效果显著降低：基线为 33–47/75，CapScope 为 3/75；同时修复完成率保持在 68/75，与基线的 68–72/75 相近。

Conclusion: CapScope 证明了无需依赖模型识别恶意文本，也可以通过权限隔离和调用前校验有效降低间接提示注入风险，并且对任务完成效率影响较小。

Abstract: Coding agents use system-level tools to read files, execute commands, and modify source code. Within the agent's sandbox, these tools often carry ambient authority: naming a resource is sufficient to act on it. Indirect prompt injection exploits this authority by placing instructions in repository files or tool output that cause the agent to perform actions the user did not request. We propose CapScope, a harness-level authorization mechanism that restricts tool use without requiring the model to identify malicious text. Before repository contents or tool output are read, CapScope derives a task-wide authority ceiling from trusted input. It then assigns each agent a separate set of typed capabilities, stored outside the model's context. Every tool call is checked against the capabilities of the agent that issued it. Permissions assigned to one sub-agent are therefore not automatically available to another. An injection may cause an agent to request an action, but the request is blocked unless that agent already has the required capability. We implement CapScope on the Pi coding agent and evaluate it in a repair workflow where an orchestrator delegates subtasks to separate sub-agents. The evaluation covers five Python tasks, five injection surfaces, four authorization conditions, and three trials per cell (300 runs). The injected effect executes in 33-47/75 runs under the ambient-authority and global-policy baselines, compared with 3/75 under CapScope. CapScope completes 68/75 repairs, while the baselines complete 68-72/75.

</details>


### [108] [PLC-Bin2Src: Retrieving Corresponding Structured Text Source Files for PLC Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08563)
*Ang Jia,Yaxin Duan,He Jiang,Ming Fan,Zhilei Ren,Xiaochen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 PLC-Bin2Src，用于在源码仓库中为不同 PLC 平台生成的二进制制品匹配对应的 Structured Text（ST）源码文件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件复用和供应链审计需要识别二进制组件来源，但 PLC 二进制制品通常不可读，且不同平台编译结果、二进制单元粒度和 ST 源码粒度存在差异，导致源码追踪困难。

Method: PLC-Bin2Src 采用平台感知前端，为 CODESYS、GEB、OpenPLC v2 和 OpenPLC v3 构建可比较的表示；后端综合控制—数据流图（CDFG）、函数调用图（FCG）和恢复符号相似度，对候选 ST 源码进行排序。

Result: 在 PLC-BEAD 数据集上，该方法在四个 PLC 平台上取得 95.89% Recall@1、99.66% Recall@5，MRR 为 0.9769，说明其能够较准确地定位对应源码。

Conclusion: 该框架通过融合多类结构特征与符号信息，有效缓解 PLC 二进制与 ST 源码之间的表示和粒度差异，为 PLC 软件组成分析、依赖盘点、漏洞评估和安全审计提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Software reuse allows existing components and third-party libraries to be incorporated into new applications, but binary-only components can obscure their origins and implementations. Software composition analysis seeks to identify these reused components and trace their provenance, supporting dependency inventory, vulnerability assessment, and security auditing. For PLC applications, binary2source matching provides a core link in this analysis: given an opaque PLC binary artifact, retrieve its corresponding Structured Text (ST) source file from a collected source repository. However, this task is complicated by cross-platform compilation heterogeneity, the representation gap between PLC binaries and ST source code, and the granularity mismatch between recovered binary units and ST source files. This paper presents PLC-Bin2Src, a cross-platform binary2source matching framework for retrieving corresponding ST source files for binaries produced by CODESYS, GEB, OpenPLC v2, and OpenPLC v3. Platform-aware frontends construct comparable representations, and a shared backend equally combines control--data flow graph (CDFG), function call graph (FCG), and recovered-symbol similarities to rank source candidates. We evaluate PLC-Bin2Src on PLC-BEAD. Results show that PLC-Bin2Src achieves 95.89% Recall@1, 99.66% Recall@5, and an MRR of 0.9769 across four PLC platforms.

</details>


### [109] [The Unreliable Progress Bar: Can LLM Agents Reliably Report Task Progress Throughout Execution?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08589)
*Boyang Wang,Yunhan Wang,Yalun Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究系统评估大语言模型在任务各阶段报告进度的可靠性，发现多数模型在任务中段报告准确率下降，最新模型则在结束阶段变得保守，提示智能体框架不应仅依赖模型状态报告控制任务流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架依赖模型发出的任务进度信号决定继续或停止，但模型在各阶段报告进度的可靠性及其失效模式尚未系统研究。

Method: 在公开基准 τ²-bench 和受控测试平台 StageIF 上评测多个已部署模型，两者均要求在任务生命周期的多个检查点报告进度。

Result: 报告可靠性随任务阶段变化：大多数模型在任务进行中准确率下降、任务完成后恢复；最新一代模型消除了中段下降，但在结束阶段表现过度保守。

Conclusion: 任务进度报告存在能力缺口，智能体框架不应仅凭模型的状态报告来控制任务流转，需引入更稳健的评估与控制机制。

Abstract: Recent large language models can emit task-progress signals that agent frameworks use to decide whether a task should continue or stop, yet whether a model can reliably report its task progress at every stage of a task, and where and how its reports fail, has not been studied systematically. We evaluate this ability on the public benchmark $τ^2$-bench and on StageIF, a controlled testbed in which reporting checkpoints are placed across the task's lifecycle. Both settings require reports at multiple task stages. We find that reporting reliability depends on the stage a task has reached, and that almost every deployed model we test is reliable at some stages and unreliable at others. Where reporting breaks down is not the same everywhere. Most deployed models lose accuracy once work is under way and recover once the task is done. The newest generation closes that mid-task drop and instead grows conservative at the finish line. Our study exposes a capability gap in task-progress reporting and provides an evaluation protocol that spans the whole course of task execution for this ability on which agent operation depends. The findings indicate that agent frameworks should not control task flow on the strength of the model's state reports alone.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Fixed Fault Models: Comparing LLM-Based and Rule-Based Fault Injection in OpenStack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08681)
*Giuseppe De Rosa,Pietro Liguori,Domenico Cotroneo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究对比了基于大语言模型（LLM）的软件故障注入与基于规则的注入器ProFIPy，在OpenStack Nova和Cinder服务上的表现，发现两者在激活率和可观测故障率相当，但故障表现谱不同：LLM生成的故障在Nova上产生更多灾难性结果，而ProFIPy产生更多静默和多组件影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则化故障注入器需要手动编码故障模式，局限性大；LLM提供数据驱动的上下文相关故障生成，可能扩展行为覆盖，因此需评估其实际效果。

Method: 选取两个代码LLM（Qwen2.5-Coder和DeepSeek-Coder）与ProFIPy，在OpenStack Nova和Cinder服务的共享注入目标上进行对比实验，测量激活率、可观测故障率、故障后果分类及传播范围。

Result: 激活率和可观测故障率相当；LLM故障在Nova上产生更多灾难性结果，ProFIPy产生更多静默和多组件效应；两LLM输出在故障表现方式上差异较大，但在传播范围上一致性较高。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的故障注入扩展了固定故障模型的行为覆盖，但未确立整体优势；实际采用仍需受控生成、运行时验证、系统级预言机及可复现的实验溯源。

Abstract: Software Fault Injection (SFI) supports testing of cloud systems by introducing software defects and observing their manifestation. Rule-based injectors such as ProFIPy provide controlled and reproducible source-level mutations but require fault patterns to be encoded manually. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a data-driven alternative by generating context-dependent software faults. We compare two code LLMs, Qwen2.5-Coder and DeepSeek-Coder, with ProFIPy in OpenStack's Nova and Cinder services. On shared injection targets, activation and observable-failure rates are comparable, but operational profiles differ: LLM-generated faults produce more Catastrophic outcomes on Nova, whereas ProFIPy produces more Silent and Multi-component effects. The sampled LLM outputs also differ in how they manifest failure, while showing greater agreement in their propagation scope. These findings show that LLM-based fault injection extends the behavioral coverage of fixed fault models without establishing general superiority, and that practical adoption still requires controlled generation, runtime validation, system-level oracles, and reproducible experimental provenance.

</details>


### [111] [DJPlus: Generating minimal test suites for strong coverage criteria in graph models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.08953)
*Yavuz Köroğlu,Mutlu Beyazıt,Onur Kılınççeker,Serge Demeyer,Franz Wotawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 DJPlus，一种面向图模型的自动化测试生成优化方法，可在满足图覆盖要求的同时减少测试步骤和执行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型驱动测试中，图覆盖能提升测试套件强度，但往往导致测试用例过长、执行成本高，尤其在工业规模系统中需要进一步压缩冗余步骤。

Method: 作者将测试生成建模为优化问题，设计 DJPlus 方法，在满足给定图覆盖准则的条件下生成更精简的测试套件；并实现验证 edge-pair 等覆盖准则，与替代方法进行对比。

Result: 在四个真实系统上，DJPlus 展示了 edge-pair 覆盖准则的可行性；prime path 准则暴露出可扩展性问题。替代方法产生的冗余测试步骤比 DJPlus 多 2 到 26 倍，DJPlus 通过减少测试步骤降低执行时间。

Conclusion: DJPlus 能够有效减少满足图覆盖要求所需的测试步骤，提升模型驱动测试的效率，为工业规模测试生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Automated test generation from graph models is essential to model-based testing. In this type of testing, graph coverage ensures test suite strength but also results in long test cases that take time to execute on the system under test. We propose a novel optimization-driven method, DJPlus, which generates reduced test suites while satisfying given graph-based test requirements. We implement DJPlus and show the feasibility of edge-pair criterion, a stronger coverage criterion than vertex or edge criteria, on four realistic systems, while prime path criterion poses scalability issues. Our evaluation reveals that the alternative methods generate 2 to 26 times more redundant test steps than DJPlus and DJPlus decreases test execution times by reducing the number of test steps. These results show that DJPlus is a positive step towards tackling the challenges of model-based testing at an industrial scale.

</details>


### [112] [It Is Not My Code Anymore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09022)
*Augusto Camargo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了AI辅助编程中代码生产者、所有权和责任归属的问题，强调质量工程应评估生成的实现和其产生过程，并提出区分与评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI在软件开发中的应用日益普及，如何界定代码的生产者、所有权以及失败时的责任成为重要伦理和实践问题。本文旨在通过分析这些区别，为AI辅助软件生产提供理论框架和评估指导。

Method: 通过假设性入学失败场景和对文献的选择性阅读，分析代码生产者的身份如何影响审查者、发布决策者和服务操作员的职责，同时考察质量工程如何评估生成的实现和其产生过程。

Result: 研究发现，仅识别代码的生产者并不能自动确定相关方的职责；集体所有权也无法明确职责分配。质量工程可以同时评估生成的实现和其产生的流程，接受准则应基于所需的服务结果，组件检查可为该结果提供证据支持。同时，比较那些不生成独立程序而直接完成任务的系统，可以改变作者身份但保持服务义务不变。

Conclusion: 本文没有提供新的实证结果，而是提出了关于AI辅助软件生产的区分概念和评估问题，强调需要明确责任边界并建立合理的接受标准，以确保服务质量。

Abstract: AI-assisted programming raises distinct questions about who produces code, who feels ownership of it, and who is responsible when it fails. This research note examines these distinctions through a hypothetical enrollment failure and a selective reading of the literature. Identifying the producer of a defective expression does not, by itself, determine the duties of reviewers, release decision-makers, or service operators. Collective ownership likewise leaves those duties to be specified. The discussion then considers how quality engineering can evaluate both generated implementations and the processes that produce them. Acceptance criteria should be justified by the required service outcome, with component checks contributing evidence toward that outcome. This perspective also permits comparison with systems that perform a task without generating a separate program for it. Such substitution would change the object of authorship while leaving the service obligation intact. The note reports no new empirical results; it proposes distinctions and evaluation questions for AI-assisted software production.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [The 2M Multiplication Algorithm for Complex Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05419)
*Peter Caday*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出一种新的复数矩阵乘法方法：对整数实部和虚部，可用2次同尺寸实数GEMM完成复数GEMM，加上二次时间的预处理和后处理；对浮点数，则结合Ozaki-II方案获得接近2倍实数GEMM时间的实用高性能算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复数矩阵乘法需要4次实数矩阵乘法；已有3M算法虽将成本降至3次实数GEMM，但仍需额外的二次时间预处理和后处理。作者希望进一步降低计算量，并让复数浮点矩阵乘法更高效地利用BLAS/GEMM。

Method: 作者将3M乘法重新组织为2M形式，利用整数实部和虚部结构，通过2次实数GEMM完成复数乘法；对于浮点输入，则与Ozaki-II补偿/稳定化方案结合，以兼顾性能和数值可靠性。同时，该方法被用于构造新的对称秩k更新算法。

Result: 对于整数实部和虚部的复数矩阵，论文实现了仅用2次实数GEMM的复数矩阵乘法；对于浮点矩阵，结合Ozaki-II后，可在实际应用中达到约两倍单次实数GEMM时间的复杂度表现。此外，作者还给出了新的SYRK/HERK算法，其内部使用完整矩形GEMM。

Conclusion: 该工作的核心贡献是将复数矩阵乘法从3M进一步降到2M，并展示了其在整数和浮点场景下的实用价值，尤其适合依赖高性能GEMM的线性代数软件栈。

Abstract: Complex matrix multiplication is typically computed using 4 real matrix multiplications (GEMMs) of the same size. The well-known 3M multiplication algorithm reduces this cost to 3 real GEMMs, together with quadratic time pre- and post-processing steps.
  In this paper, we reduce 3M to 2M for matrices with integer real and imaginary parts, performing complex GEMM with only 2 real GEMMs of the same size, along with quadratic time pre- and post-processing. For floating-point matrices, 2M multiplication combines naturally with the Ozaki-II scheme, yielding a practical, high-performance algorithm for computing a complex floating-point GEMM in roughly twice the time of a real GEMM of the same size. As corollaries, we derive new algorithms for symmetric rank-$k$ updates (SYRK/HERK) that internally use full rectangular GEMMs.

</details>


### [114] [DejaVu: Unifying Memory Allocations to Eliminate Redundant Copies on Unified-Memory SoCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05635)
*Yuheng Zhu,Yanbo Zhao,Jiajia Li,Man-Ki Yoon*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DejaVu 是一种针对统一内存（UMA）边缘平台的优化工具，旨在消除不必要的 CPU-GPU 数据复制。它通过编译时（DejaVu-SR）和运行时（DejaVu-DR）两种互补路径，在确保程序安全和访问顺序的前提下合并冗余缓冲区，从而显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 应用在 UMA 平台上通常保留了独立 GPU 的内存抽象，分别为 CPU 和 GPU 分配独立缓冲区并进行数据复制。由于物理上它们位于同一内存中，这些复制操作浪费了带宽、时间和能量。但直接移除复制很困难，因为程序可能依赖双缓冲区的独立性或复制操作带来的 CPU/GPU 访问顺序。

Method: DejaVu 根据源码是否可用提供两种方案：DejaVu-SR 是编译时 LLVM 变换，通过证明安全性后就地重写缓冲区对；DejaVu-DR 是针对闭源部署的 profile 引导二进制优化器，通过运行时拦截匹配的调用，合并已验证的稳定分配/复制模式，并保留原有的排序效应。

Result: 在三个 NVIDIA Jetson 平台的七个基准测试中，复制主导型工作负载加速高达 6.9 倍，闭源端到端应用加速 1.10-1.14 倍。两种路径均达到了手动优化性能的 99% 以上。

Conclusion: DejaVu 能够安全、高效地自动消除 UMA 平台上的冗余数据复制，大幅缩短开发周期并提升性能，其效果可媲美手动优化。

Abstract: GPU applications on unified-memory (UMA) edge platforms often inherit a discrete-GPU memory abstraction in which they allocate one buffer for the CPU, another for the GPU, and copy data between them before and after GPU execution. On UMA hardware these buffers reside in the same physical DRAM, so the copies consume bandwidth, time, and energy without moving data across a physical boundary. Despite the growing adoption of UMA platforms, this pattern remains common because production software stacks, libraries, and samples were written for portability across discrete GPUs. However, removing these copies is not as simple as merging the two buffers, because the original program may rely on the two buffers being distinct, or on the copy itself ordering CPU and GPU accesses.
  DejaVu removes these copies only when the program does not depend on the effects above. It does so along two complementary paths, depending on whether source is available. DejaVu-SR is a compile-time LLVM transformation that proves safety and rewrites accepted pairs in place. DejaVu-DR is a profile-guided binary optimizer for closed-source deployments that profiles and validates stable allocation/copy patterns and, at runtime, intercepts the matching calls to coalesce profile-matched pairs while preserving the ordering effects of removed copies.
  Across seven benchmarks on three NVIDIA Jetson platforms, DejaVu's benefit grows with the fraction of baseline time spent on copies. Copy-dominated workloads speed up by up to 6.9$\times$, closed-source end-to-end applications speed up by 1.10-1.14$\times$. Both source and binary paths achieve $\ge$99% of the performance achievable by manual optimization.

</details>


### [115] [Sustained 70B-Class AWQ Inference on a Single NVIDIA L20: Throughput, Stability, Energy, and Quality Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05420)
*Yin Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本技术报告评估了在单张 NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU 上使用 vLLM 和 AWQ 量化技术部署 70B 级模型 Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ 的可行性。实验表明，在特定固定工作负载下，单张 L20 能够作为高吞吐量的 70B 级推理端点稳定运行，但其性能与无损质量、低延迟交互及全场景 SLA 仍有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，部署 70B 级开源大模型通常需要 80GB 显存的加速器或多 GPU 集群。本研究旨在探索是否能仅凭单张 48GB 显存的 NVIDIA L20 GPU，结合 AWQ 量化技术，提供一种高性价比的 70B 级模型推理服务方案。

Method: 在单张 L20 48GB GPU 上，利用 vLLM 0.8.5.post1 和 AWQ Marlin 部署 Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ。通过固定工作负载（512 输入 / 256 输出 token）进行 24 小时并发为 10 的稳定性测试，并在并发 1、4、8、16 下进行重复基准测试。同时监测了吞吐量、延迟（首 token 延迟及端到端延迟）、功耗，并评估了 MMLU、CMMLU、GSM8K、MT-Bench 和 LongBench 等基准的推理质量。

Result: 系统在 24 小时测试中无故障完成了全部 36,740 个请求，无 OOM 或崩溃。吞吐量达 108.84 输出 tokens/s（并发 16 时达 127.22 tokens/s），p95 首 token 延迟 6.61s，p95 端到端延迟 23.54s。能效为 0.330 输出 tokens/J。量化模型在 MMLU（0.8130）、CMMLU（0.8309）、GSM8K（0.8082）等测试中取得了优秀的质量分数。

Conclusion: 单张 NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU 能够在特定固定形状的吞吐量导向工作负载下，稳定运行量化版 Qwen2.5-72B 模型。但此结论仅限于特定测试条件，不能推广至无损量化质量验证、低延迟交互式服务、广泛的生产级 SLA 覆盖或等同于 BF16/FP16 的全精度推理。

Abstract: Serving 70B-class open-weight language models is usually associated with 80GB accelerators, tensor-parallel multi-GPU systems, or vendor-managed inference profiles. This technical report evaluates whether a single NVIDIA L20 48GB GPU can sustain a useful 70B-class quantized serving workload. We measure Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ served with vLLM 0.8.5.post1 and AWQ Marlin on one L20. Under a fixed workload of approximately 512 input tokens and 256 output tokens, a 24-hour concurrency-10 soak completed 36,740/36,740 requests with no request failures and no vLLM CUDA OOM, traceback, or killed-process signatures. The system sustained 108.84 output tokens/s, with p95 time-to-first-token of 6.61s and p95 end-to-end latency of 23.54s. GPU-board power sampled through nvidia-smi produced an estimated 7.92 kWh over the run, corresponding to 0.330 output tokens/J and 1.008 total tokens/J. Repeated fixed-shape runs at concurrency 1, 4, 8, and 16 completed 12/12 runs successfully; the concurrency-16 condition averaged 127.22 +/- 12.68 output tokens/s over three runs. The same AWQ endpoint also produced absolute quality scores of 0.8130 on MMLU, 0.8309 on CMMLU, and 0.8082 on GSM8K, plus 80/80 MT-Bench answer generations and a 60-item 8K LongBench subset. The evidence supports a narrow claim: a carefully configured single L20 can serve Qwen2.5-72B-Instruct-AWQ as a throughput-oriented 70B-class endpoint under the tested fixed-shape workload. It does not prove lossless AWQ quality retention, low-latency interactive serving, broad production SLA coverage, or equivalence to a BF16/FP16 baseline.

</details>


### [116] [Toward a Formally Verified Optimality Certificate for OGR(29): A SAT-Encoding Methods Note with Small-Case Demos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05421)
*Tong Niu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究最优Golomb尺问题 OGR(n)，报道了 OGR(29) 的最新上界 OGR(29)≤757，并提出了 O(n²L) 子句的 CNF 编码、LRAT 证书验证管线及 cube-and-conquer 并行策略，旨在通过有限元方法实现首个非 trivial n 的正式最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OGR(n) 作为一个经典的组合组合基准，其计算难度随 n 指数级上升，已知值仅至 n=28，n=29 正处于分布式计算验证阶段。本工程为突破计算瓶颈、实现首个正式最优性证明提供了编码与验证框架。

Method: 提出决策问题 GR(n,L) 的紧凑 CNF 编码，子句数为 O(n²L)；设计基于 CaDiCaL 的 LRAT 证书生成管线，并结合结构性检查与 drat-trim/cake_lpr 形式验证；提出 cube-and-conquer 并行分解策略以适应 Mallob 风格多核硬件。

Result: 针对 n≤12 的小规模情况已完成验证并产出机器检查的 LRAT 证书；对 OGR(29, L=757) 给出了闭形式编码规模估计；证管线实现了从生成到形式验证的端到端流程。

Conclusion: 任何单个 OGR(n) 的正式最优性证明（超越 trivial n≤5）将是该领域的首次成果，本文提供的编码与证书管线为该目标奠定了基础。

Abstract: A Golomb ruler of order~$n$ is an integer set $\{a_0<a_1<\cdots<a_{n-1}\}$ whose $\binom{n}{2}$ pairwise differences $a_j-a_i$ ($i<j$) are all distinct. The optimal Golomb ruler problem asks for $\mathrm{OGR}(n)=\min\{a_{n-1}-a_0\}$ and is a classical combinatorial benchmark. The values $\mathrm{OGR}(2),\dots,\mathrm{OGR}(28)$ are settled through distributed volunteer search (the Distributed.net OGR project); $\mathrm{OGR}(29)$ is in active computation, with verification expected in late 2026 or early 2027. The recent upper bound $\mathrm{OGR}(29)\le 757$ of Lee, Park, and Kim (arXiv:2510.0122, October~2025) tightens the search window. This note describes a compact CNF encoding of the decision problem $\mathrm{GR}(n,L)$ (``is there a Golomb ruler of order~$n$ with length exactly~$L$?'') with $O(n^2 L)$ clauses, together with a small-case verification sweep that emits machine-checkable LRAT certificates of optimality for $\mathrm{OGR}(n)$ at $n\le 12$. We then give closed-form encoding-size estimates for $\mathrm{OGR}(29, L=757)$ and propose a cube-and-conquer decomposition aimed at a Mallob-style parallel run on commodity multi-core hardware. The certificate pipeline (CaDiCaL with --lrat=true, a structural sanity-check, then formal validation by drat-trim or cake\_lpr) is end-to-end. A formally verified optimality proof for any single $\mathrm{OGR}(n)$ value beyond the trivial $n\le 5$ would be a first in the field.

</details>


### [117] [STORE: Self-Provisioning Storage-as-a-Service for Serverless Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05422)
*Florian Trimmel,Cynthia Marcelino,Thomas Pusztai,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 STORE 自供给存储架构，实现无服务器函数的零配置、零触碰存储后端自动选择与供给。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器函数通常无状态，依赖外部存储服务，但存储系统的供给和配置仍需手动或声明式脚本，增加复杂度、减缓开发、易出错。

Method: 将自供给逻辑移至平台层，设计 STORE 架构，自动选择最优存储后端，实现零触碰、零配置供给。

Result: 与 Terraform、Pulumi 等 IaC 框架相比，实现工作量减少高达 84%，在保持低延迟和线性扩展的同时无性能开销。

Conclusion: STORE 有效解决了无服务器函数存储供给的复杂性问题，显著降低开发负担且不牺牲性能。

Abstract: Serverless computing provides on-demand elasticity, pay-per-use, and simplified deployment. However, serverless functions are typically stateless and depend on external storage services such as object stores or databases to exchange data or support stateful functions. Provisioning and configuring these storage systems still requires manual setup or declarative scripts, introducing complexity, slowing development, and increasing the risk of configuration errors. To address these challenges, in this paper, we introduce \textsc{STORE}, a self-provisioning storage architecture for serverless functions. STORE automatically selects the optimal storage backend and eliminates developer effort through zero-touch and zero-configuration provisioning, achieved by moving the self-provisioning logic to the platform level. Our evaluation results show that STORE reduces implementation effort by up to 84% compared to well-established Infrastructure-as-Code frameworks such as Terraform and Pulumi while maintaining low latency and linear scalability under realistic workloads, without introducing performance overhead.

</details>


### [118] [A Three-Layer Caching Architecture for Low-Latency LLM Web Search on Commodity CPU Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05463)
*Ayushman Bhattacharya,Nihal Gazi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了OreoLook（原lixSearch）系统，采用三层缓存架构解决AI搜索产品在上下文丢失、重复查询和URL重复嵌入等问题，通过本地硬件运行的自动化浏览器代理和远程LLM推理实现高效的答案生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI搜索产品如ChatGPT Search、Google AI Overviews和Perplexity虽然提供了基于实时网页结果的LLM综合回答，但随着使用量增长出现了严重问题：会话上下文丢失、相似查询触发冗余工作、以及URL在不同会话中被重复嵌入，这些问题导致效率低下和资源浪费。

Method: 开发了一种三层缓存架构，包括（1）会话上下文窗口使用Redis维护最近消息的滚动窗口，并自动溢出到Huffman压缩的磁盘归档；（2）语义查询缓存通过向量嵌入的余弦相似度捕捉同义查询，消除冗余LLM调用；（3）URL嵌入缓存去重跨会话的嵌入计算。系统部署在单台8vCPU Intel Cascade Lake服务器上，使用30个Hypercorn工作进程和三个容器化副本。

Result: 在测试中，该系统实现了89.3%的Redis键空间命中率，读延迟为0.1毫秒，内存开销仅1.38MB。一个后台LRU淘汰守护进程将空闲会话从Redis迁移到磁盘并按需重新激活，使得对话可以在几小时或几天后恢复。

Conclusion: 该研究通过创新的多层缓存设计有效解决了AI搜索系统中的上下文管理和冗余计算问题，在保持低延迟和低内存开销的同时显著提升了系统效率，为构建高性能的本地化AI搜索引擎提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI-powered search products such as ChatGPT search, Google's AI Overviews, and Perplexity provide LLM-synthesized answers grounded in live web results. We developed OreoLook (formerly lixSearch), an open-source answer engine using automated browser agents and provider-routed LLM inference. Its local search, caching, session-management, and embedding stack runs on commodity CPU hardware; answer synthesis is performed by a remote inference provider. As usage grew, sessions lost context, equivalent queries triggered redundant work, and URLs were repeatedly embedded across sessions.
  We present a three-layer caching architecture: (1) a Session Context Window maintaining a rolling window of recent messages in Redis with automatic overflow to Huffman-compressed disk archives; (2) a Semantic Query Cache catches rephrasings via cosine similarity on embedding vectors, eliminating redundant LLM invocations; and (3) a URL Embedding Cache that deduplicates embedding computations across sessions. Deployed on a single 8-vCPU Intel Cascade Lake server (2 GHz, 32 GB RAM) running 30 Hypercorn worker processes across three containerized replicas, the evaluated system reported an 89.3% aggregate Redis keyspace hit rate with 0.1 ms read latency and just 1.38 MB of memory overhead. A background LRU eviction daemon migrates idle sessions from Redis to disk and re-hydrates them on demand, enabling conversations that can be resumed hours or days later under the configured retention policy.

</details>


### [119] [Research on Intra-Chip Fusion Deployment and Optimization of Embodied Intelligence Business Operator NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05424)
*Yuchen Zhu,Longxiang Yin,Wanyu Wang,Jieke Lin,Guoqiang Zou,Zirui Cao,Yuling Yuan,Xiaolan Fan,Lifen Chen,Hao Zheng,Qizhang He,Hongyu Zhou,Chunhai Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套基于Phytium FT-2000/4处理器和Cambricon MLU370加速卡的本土异构计算平台，实现感知、计算与控制的NPU芯片级融合部署和全流程协同优化策略。通过硬件适配、轻量化推理优化和多自由度逆运动学定制化开发，实现单帧端到端延迟18.7ms，相较于NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier速度提升2.89倍，MLU利用率达82.6%，为本土智能体机器人应用提供了实用的集成解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统将感知、计算和控制分离部署的方式导致频繁的数据传输、高延迟和低硬件效率，无法满足动态场景下毫秒级实时需求。同时，大多数优化方法依赖外接GPU平台，而对Phytium-Cambricon本土异构架构的全流程协同优化仍不足，这限制了本土智能系统在实际工程中的应用潜力。

Method: 构建基于Phytium FT-2000/4处理器和Cambricon MLU370加速卡的本土异构计算平台，采用NPU芯片级融合部署实现感知-计算-控制管道的统一执行。针对具体任务进行模块化优化：一是对MLU硬件进行运动模糊校正算子适配以支持高速成像；二是对ViT模型进行轻量化推理优化；三是开发定制化的多自由度逆运动学解算算子。采用基于单卡的开环数据闭环和流水线协作机制，实现所有感知-计算-控制任务的集成执行。

Result: 实验结果显示，所提方法实现了单帧端到端延迟18.7毫秒，相较于NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier实现了2.89倍的加速比，MLU利用率达到82.6%，且在精度上与主流平台相当。这一成果为本土工程应用中集成式智能体机器人服务提供了可行的技术参考。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了适合本土环境的异构计算平台，通过硬件-软件协同设计和全流程优化策略，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低了延迟和资源消耗，为未来更多本土智能体机器人系统的研发提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent computing integrates perception, computation and control. Traditional separate deployment of the three tasks leads to frequent data transmission, high latency and low hardware efficiency, failing to satisfy millisecond-level real-time requirements in dynamic scenarios. Besides, most operator optimization methods rely on foreign GPU platforms, while full-process collaborative optimization for domestic Phytium-Cambricon heterogeneous architectures is insufficient. This paper builds a domestic heterogeneous computing platform with Phytium FT-2000/4 processor and Cambricon MLU370 acceleration card, and proposes an NPU on-chip fusion deployment and full-process operator collaborative optimization strategy for perception, computation and control pipelines. Targeting embodied robot applications, modular optimization is conducted, including MLU hardware adaptation of motion blur correction operators for high-speed imaging, lightweight inference optimization of ViT models, and customized operator development for multi-DOF inverse kinematics solution. An on-chip data closed-loop and pipeline collaboration-based single-card solution is proposed to implement integrated execution of all perception-computation-control tasks on MLU370. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves a full-process single-frame latency of 18.7 ms and a speedup of 2.89 compared with NVIDIA Jetson AGX Xavier, with 82.6% MLU utilization and comparable accuracy to mainstream platforms. This work offers a practical reference for domestic engineering applications of integrated embodied intelligent computing services.

</details>


### [120] [Measurement-Driven Diagnosis and Mitigation of Host-CPU Co-location Interference in Single-GPU LLM Serving on a Multi-GPU Server](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05425)
*Guanjie Cheng,Guowei Li,Yingying Wen,Xinkui Zhao,Zhe Liu,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文揭示宿主CPU协同定位在单GPU LLM服务中的主要干扰源是CPU侧服务阶段的长尾效应而非GPU内核慢down，提出核心路径尾指数(CPTI)和核心尾抑制(CTS)两大指标，并设计了测量驱动的CoTail诊断流程，有效筛选风险并选择OS级保护，显著提升协同定位下的 serving 质量与SLO合规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU服务器中的宿主CPU often在LLM推理中闲置，协同定位可提升资源利用率，但可能严重损害服务质量。现有工作多聚焦LLM服务引擎或CPU-GPU边界延迟，缺乏对外部CPU工作负载如何影响服务路径以及如何选择保护策略的指引。

Method: 引入核心路径尾指数(CPTI)与核心尾抑制(CTS)度量，构建CoTail测量驱动诊断流程，实现工作负载风险筛选、服务阶段尾部分析、OS级保护选择以及解码SLO合规性验证。

Result: 实验显示，未受保护的nginx协同定位使吞吐量下降78.8%，TTFT激增429.5%，TPOT上升362.4%。CoTail引导的保护使nginx吞吐量提升至4.4倍，TPOT降低4.5倍。在常见基线SLO下，CoTail满足12/12 oracle-feasible held-out情况，优于Always-rt(10/12)和Macro-only(11/12)，并将RT使用从28降至22，平均共 tenant slowdown从56.65%降至51.21%。

Conclusion: CoTail通过精准诊断CPU侧长尾效应并选择适配的OS级保护，有效缓解协同定位干扰，在保证SLO合规的同时显著提升资源利用率和 serving 性能。

Abstract: Host CPUs in GPU servers are often under-used during LLM inference. Co-locating CPU workloads can improve resource use, but it can also seriously hurt serving quality. Existing work mainly improves LLM serving engines or studies CPU-GPU boundary delays. It gives limited guidance on how external CPU workloads affect the serving path and how operators should choose protection policies. This paper studies host-CPU co-location interference in single-GPU LLM serving. We show that the main observed problem is not slower GPU kernels. Instead, CPU workloads amplify long tails in CPU-side serving stages before GPU work is submitted. To capture this effect, we introduce the Core Path Tail Index (CPTI) and Core Tail Suppression (CTS). Based on these metrics, we build CoTail, a measurement-driven diagnostic procedure that screens workload risk, profiles serving-stage tails, selects OS-level protections, and validates decode SLO compliance. In our primary setup, unprotected nginx co-location reduces throughput by 78.8%, increases TTFT by 429.5%, and increases TPOT by 362.4%. CoTail-guided protections improve nginx throughput by up to 4.4x and reduce TPOT by 4.5x. Under a common-baseline deployment SLO, CoTail satisfies all 12 oracle-feasible held-out cases, compared with 10/12 for Always-rt and 11/12 for Macro-only. It also reduces RT usage from 28 to 22 cases and lowers mean co-tenant slowdown from 56.65% to 51.21%.

</details>


### [121] [Sharing a Fabric with Collective Communication: Two Storage Penalties in Deep Learning Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06506)
*Chen Wang,Wenzhao Wu,Hyojin Kim,Jae-Sung Yeom*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 在共享网络Fabric的HPC系统中，分布式DL训练因DataLoader的重尾阻塞和集体通信的流量争用导致性能下降，使用DYAD在本地NVMe上缓存数据可消除这两类开销，实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式DL训练在HPC环境中因共享网络Fabric导致I/O阻塞和通信争用，需要通过本地缓存降低这两类成本以提升训练效率。

Method: 通过DYAD在节点本地NVMe上进行数据缓存（staging），将存储I/O路径从共享Fabric转移至本地高速存储，从而避免重尾DataLoader等待和所有reduce的网络拥塞。

Result: DYAD在整个训练 epoch 实现7.4倍速度提升（相较于直接Lustre读取），1.06倍提升（相较于VAST），第二个 epoch后 stalls完全消除，达到1.31倍速度提升，显著缩短训练时间。

Conclusion: 本地NVMe缓存能够消除I/O阻塞和通信争用，使分布式DL训练在共享网络Fabric上获得显著性能提升，证明DYAD的有效性。

Abstract: Distributed DL training on HPC systems often shares one network fabric between NCCL/RCCL collective communication and parallel-filesystem I/O. Using a real GNN training workload on a Slingshot-11 system, we show that this sharing imposes two distinct costs. The primary cost is heavy-tailed DataLoader stalls: the typical DataLoader wait is just 15 ms at steady state, yet spikes to multiple seconds in 28% of Lustre iterations and 12% of VAST iterations. The secondary cost is traffic-class contention on collective communication: Lustre I/O stalls the all-reduce by up to 145$\times$ in an isolated benchmark. The two costs arise from different mechanisms. I/O stall latency affects any storage path that traverses the shared fabric, whereas all-reduce network contention occurs only when storage and collective communication share the same traffic class. Their common root cause is that storage I/O traverses the shared fabric. This work shows that node-local NVMe staging via DYAD (Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/flux-framework/dyad) eliminates both effects by keeping storage I/O off that path. Across a full training epoch, DYAD achieves a 7.4 times speedup over direct Lustre reads and a 1.06 times speedup over VAST. By the second epoch, once the local cache is fully warmed, DataLoader stalls are eliminated entirely, allowing DYAD to reach a 1.31 times speedup over VAST.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Lemma Sharing -- Novel Parallelization Strategies for Property Directed Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05426)
*Verner Vlačić*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了两种基于共享的并行化策略（预emptive传播和ARPOS），并在rIC3硬件模型检查器上验证，发现预emptive传播策略显著优于经典的lemma共享方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Property Directed Reachability (PDR)是自动化硬件模型检查中常用技术，但现有并行化方法如lemma共享在处理大规模问题时存在可扩展性限制，难以充分利用多核处理器资源。

Method: 开发了一个异步MPI基于消息传递框架，对状态为前沿的rIC3硬件模型检查器进行改造，提出了两种新的共享并行化策略：预emptive传播和ARPOS，并与传统的lemma共享方法进行了比较评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，在2025年硬件模型检查竞赛基准测试上，预emptive传播策略相比于经典的lemma共享方法实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 该研究成功展示了两种新的并行化策略，其中预emptive传播策略表现出色，为硬件模型检查的自动化加速提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Property Directed Reachability (PDR) is a commonly used technique for automated hardware model checking, yet efficiently parallelizing it remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches, such as lemma sharing, often suffer from limited scalability as processor counts increase. In this work, we present two novel sharing-based parallelization strategies, preemptive propagation and ARPOS, and compare their performance with classical lemma sharing. To this end, we develop an asynchronous MPI-based message passing framework for the state-of-the-art rIC3 hardware model checker. Experimental results on the 2025 Hardware Model Checking competition benchmark demonstrate that our preemptive propagation strategy yields a significant performance boost over classical lemma sharing.

</details>


### [123] [Scalability Analysis of Distributed Kolmogorov-Arnold Network Training on High-Performance Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.07740)
*Guangneng Chen,David Garcia Selfa,Pablo Quesada Barriuso*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对 Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks（KAN）在多节点、多 GPU 环境下的数据并行训练进行了实证可扩展性研究，重点评估强扩展、弱扩展、通信开销和模型规模扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN 用边上的可学习一元函数替代 MLP 中固定激活函数和线性权重，在可解释性和参数效率方面具有潜力；但现有研究更多关注其逼近能力，缺乏对分布式多 GPU 训练行为的系统分析。

Method: 作者在 FinisTerrae III 超级计算机上，使用最多 4 个节点、8 张 NVIDIA A100 GPU 和 PyTorch Distributed Data Parallel（DDP）训练 KAN，并从强扩展、弱扩展、通信开销、模型规模扩展四个维度进行实验评估。

Result: 在 8 GPU 上，KAN 训练达到 74.7% 的并行效率和 5.97 倍加速，表现接近传统深度学习负载。弱扩展中单 GPU 到多 GPU 初始吞吐下降，但之后保持稳定。通信开销在 1.3% 到 6.1% 之间，主要受 All-Reduce 算法选择和节点间延迟影响，而不是 KAN 的边级梯度结构。随着模型规模增大，参数与显存比改善，但训练时间扩展不理想。

Conclusion: 研究表明，KAN 的算子级优化和数据并行优化是互补的。论文给出了 GPU 拓扑选择和模型规模选择的部署建议，同时也指出当前实验主要基于合成回归任务，结论在更复杂真实任务中的普适性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed activation functions and linear weights of Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) with learnable univariate functions on network edges, offering improved interpretability and, in some settings, competitive parameter efficiency. While the approximation properties of KANs have received considerable attention, their behavior under distributed, multi-GPU training has not been systematically characterized. This paper presents an empirical scalability study of data-parallel KAN training on multi-node, multi-GPU high-performance computing (HPC) infrastructure, evaluated along four dimensions: strong scaling, weak scaling, communication overhead, and model-size scaling.
  Experiments were conducted on the FinisTerrae III supercomputer using up to 8 NVIDIA A100 GPUs across 4 nodes with PyTorch Distributed Data Parallel (DDP). KAN training reaches 74.7% parallel efficiency at 8 GPUs with a 5.97x speedup, consistent with conventional deep learning workloads. Weak scaling shows an initial single-to-multi-GPU throughput drop followed by strong stability. Communication overhead follows a non-monotonic pattern (1.3%-6.1%), driven primarily by All-Reduce algorithm selection and inter-node latency rather than KAN's edge-wise gradient structure. The parameter-to-memory ratio improves with model size even as training time scales unfavorably.
  These results indicate that operator-level and data-parallel optimizations for KAN are complementary. We provide deployment guidelines for GPU topology and model-size selection, and discuss the limitations of a synthetic-regression evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Constella: A Novel Framework for Cost-Efficient Distributed AI Inference in LEO Space Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05427)
*Andrija Stanisic,Milos Gravara,Juan Luis Herrera,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LEO卫星星座中的分布式AI推理框架Constella，通过DNN分割和自适应路由，在降低系统成本两个数量级的同时，将推理延迟降低2.7倍，并保持≥81.9%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座作为可扩展的空间数据中心可运行AI workload，但异构卫星角色的成本优化具有挑战性，特别是确定处理器和通信卫星的比例以平衡成本与效率。

Method: 提出Constella框架，包含离线资源标识符确定最优处理器-通信卫星比例，以及在线调度算法利用星座遥测自适应地在星间和地面站路由数据，利用DNN分割实现分布式AI推理。

Result: 实验表明，相比其他方法，系统成本降低两个数量级，端到端推理延迟降低2.7倍，推理成功率不低于81.9%。

Conclusion: Constella通过DNN分割和自适应路由，实现了LEO卫星星座中成本高效的AI推理，为空间数据中心的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Space data centers built from Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are gaining increasing attention as a scalable computing infrastructure. With access to abundant solar energy and high-throughput optical inter-satellite links, such constellations can run AI workloads directly in orbit, enabling new in-space application types while optimizing existing ones such as Earth observation. However, managing satellite constellations that combine heterogeneous satellite roles introduces a cost optimization challenge. Determining the appropriate constellation size and satellite role ratio for a given workload is challenging, as over-provisioning processing satellites increases system cost, while under-provisioning limits system efficiency. To enable cost-efficient execution of AI inference workloads in such space data centers, we present Constella, a novel framework that leverages DNN splitting for distributed AI inference in LEO satellite constellations. Constella comprises an offline resource identifier that determines the optimal ratio of processor-to-communicator satellites and an online assignment algorithm. The algorithm utilizes constellation telemetry to adaptively route data within the constellation and to ground stations. We evaluate Constella on a real-world satellite dataset across scenarios of increasing complexity. Results demonstrate a reduction in system cost by up to two orders of magnitude and lower end-to-end inference latency by up to 2.7x compared to other approaches, while maintaining no less than 81.9% inference success rate.

</details>


### [125] [Grid Checkpointing: an Innovative Rollback Support for Speculative Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05428)
*Andrea Mazzucchi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出网格检查点技术，实现无需应用修改的增量检查点，几乎无运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推测性模拟需要低开销的增量检查点，以避免记录触发内存地址和复杂的内存映射管理带来的额外负担。

Method: 将对象状态所在的内存区域划分为网格，分离内存更新的拦截与内存映射管理，仅使用极少量的检测指令追踪更新，而不记录具体的内存地址。

Result: 将该方案嵌入到开源的PARSIR并行模拟运行器中，实验表明其运行时开销几乎可以忽略不计，优于现有方案。

Conclusion: 网格检查点提供了一种应用透明、开销极低的增量检查点解决方案，适用于推测性模拟场景。

Abstract: We present a new technique for application-transparent incremental checkpointing in speculative simulation, referred to as {\em Grid Checkpointing}. With our proposal, we fully separate the interception of memory update operations from memory-map management. This enables tracing memory updates that occur in the state of a simulation object via an extremely limited set of instrumentation instructions. At the methodological level, our proposal exploits an overlaid grid layout on the memory area used to host the chunks of an object state, and manages concise data for enabling incremental checkpointing with no need for knowing which chunks are really allocated/updated into the object state. Additionally, grid checkpointing fully avoids logging the addresses of the touched memory zones, as instead takes place in other existing proposals. We embedded our solution into the PARSIR (PARallel SImulation Runner) open source package and report the results of an experimental study that compares grid checkpointing with other solutions available in the literature. The results demonstrate that our approach enables incremental checkpointing via memory update tracing with nearly negligible runtime cost.

</details>


### [126] [Accelerating Sparse Linear Solvers in OpenFOAM using RISC-V Vector Extensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05429)
*Gabriele Ceccolini,Federico Ficarelli,Filippo Barbari,Simone Bnà,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究人员针对两个RISC-V平台（EPAC向量加速器和SG2044 CPU）优化了CFD中的稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV），并将其集成到自定义平滑器中。他们通过运行时转换数据格式，使其更适合向量处理，从而实现了显著加速。EPAC上平滑器速度提升6倍，SG2044上也提升1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CFD代码（如OpenFOAM）在现代向量化架构上表现不佳，因为它们依赖于MPI级并行性和有限的SIMD支持。OpenFOAM的矩阵表示无法满足向量处理器对连续内存访问的需求。因此，需要优化稀疏线性求解器内核，使其在新兴RISC-V硬件（包括长向量加速器和短向量CPU）上运行更高效。

Method: 1. 选择了两款不同的RISC-V平台：原型化长向量EPAC加速器和商用短向量SG2044处理器。2. 利用RISC-V向量原语优化了稀疏矩阵-向量乘法（SpMV）内核。3. 开发了一个自定义平滑器，用于CFD求解，在运行时将内部矩阵表示转换为适合向量的格式。4. 在EPAC测试芯片上进行了实验验证，并在配备SG2044处理器的Monte Cimone (MCv2)集群上进行了基准测试。

Result: - EPAC加速器上的平滑器执行速度提升了约6倍。- SG2044处理器上的平滑器执行速度提升了约1.5倍。- 结果证明，即使是传统CFD代码，也能在新兴研究和商业RISC-V硬件上实现显著加速。

Conclusion: 通过采用向量友好的数据布局和RISC-V向量指令，CFD中的稀疏求解器能够在多样化的RISC-V平台上实现显著性能提升。这为优化HPC代码以适应未来向量化体系结构提供了可行的途径。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) relies heavily on the efficiency of linear solvers based on sparse linear algebra kernels. Widely used frameworks like OpenFOAM exploit parallelism primarily at the domain decomposition level via MPI. Support for vector/SIMD architectures is limited to compiler auto-vectorization. Furthermore, support for such architectures is limited by OpenFOAM's internal matrix data format, which is intrinsically ill-suited for the contiguous memory accesses required for efficient execution on vector processors. In this work, we focused on two very different RISC-V architectures: the prototype long-vector EPAC accelerator and the commercial short-vector CPU Sophon SG2044. On these platforms, we optimized the Sparse Matrix-Vector multiplication (SpMV) using RISC-V vector intrinsics and integrated it into a custom smoother, performing a runtime conversion of internal data into a vector-friendly format. Experimental results on the EPAC test chip show a 6x speedup for the smoother; benchmarks on Monte Cimone (MCv2) cluster with the Sophon SG2044 processor achieve a 1.5x smoother speedup, proving that legacy CFD codes can be effectively accelerated on both research and commercial emerging hardware.

</details>


### [127] [Towards Decentralized Registries for Assets Metadata Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05430)
*Thomas Hardjono,Alexander Lipton*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了代币化非货币资产中的元数据注册模型，旨在通过去中心化基础设施解决传统证券存管模型在全球规模代币化中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前代币化面临缺乏可扩展的去中心化计算基础设施来管理资产元数据的困境，传统的中心化存管模型虽成熟但难以满足全球分布式控制与元数据完整性的需求。

Method: 通过分析并提出‘去中心化文物元数据注册模型’，阐述其如何延伸传统存管功能，并作为全球存管机构的新型服务方案。

Result: 提出的注册模型在概念上延展了传统CSD的职能，并被视为各国存管机构可能提供的新服务，为代币化资产的基础设施提供了新方向。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，去中心化元数据注册是金融业拥抱代币化的可行方向，有助于在分布式控制与资产完整性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The effort to tokenize non-currency assets faces several hurdles, including the lack of a scalable decentralized computing infrastructure to manage asset-related metadata. While the centralized securities depository model has served the financial industry well for several decades, the vision of tokenization at a global scale requires new infrastructure that enables distributed control while protecting the integrity of asset-related metadata, regardless of where it is stored. In this paper, we discuss the decentralized artifacts metadata registry model for tokenized assets as a possible direction for the financial industry seeking to embrace tokenization. The artifacts metadata registries extend the function of the traditional CSD, and could in fact be a new type of service offered by CSDs around the world.

</details>


### [128] [COAST: Congestion-Aware Start-Time Recommendations for Carbon-Aware HPC Jobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05443)
*Weibin Feng,Abhishek Dasgupta,Zeynep Duygu Tekler,Sudha Ahuja,Jin Zheng,Xun Jiang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出COAST，一种考虑拥塞的启动时间推荐机制，协调HPC作业以平衡碳减排与额外延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算负载耗电量大，碳排放随电网碳强度变化；无协调的转移会导致推荐时段集中，产生拥塞并降低碳效益。

Method: 将每个决策批次建模为精确势博弈，通过最佳响应更新收敛到稳定推荐；在理想化实现模型下，使用公开HPC能耗数据和历史电网碳强度轨迹进行仿真。

Result: COAST在平均额外延迟1.5小时的情况下减少碳排放15.4%；相比碳贪婪推荐，峰值时段负载降低23.8%，同时保留93%的可实现碳节省。

Conclusion: 结果量化了针对灵活、碳感知HPC作业的拥塞感知启动时间协调的潜在效益。

Abstract: High-performance computing (HPC) workloads consume substantial amounts of electricity, and their carbon emissions vary over time with the carbon intensity of grid electricity. However, uncoordinated shifting of carbon-aware HPC jobs toward low-carbon periods can concentrate recommended start times in the same time slots, creating congestion and eroding the resulting carbon benefits. This paper proposes COAST, a congestion-aware start-time recommendation mechanism that coordinates HPC jobs by balancing carbon savings against additional job delay and congestion in recommended start-time slots. COAST formulates each decision batch as an exact potential game, enabling best-response updates to converge to stable start-time recommendations. Under an idealized realization model that assumes each job can start at its recommended time, we conduct trace-driven simulations using public HPC energy data and historical grid carbon-intensity traces. The results show that COAST reduces estimated carbon emissions by 15.4\% with 1.5 hours of additional average start delay. Compared with carbon-greedy start-time recommendations, COAST reduces peak time-slot load by 23.8\% while preserving 93\% of the achievable carbon savings. These results quantify the potential benefits of congestion-aware start-time coordination for flexible, carbon-aware HPC jobs.

</details>


### [129] [Toward Sustainable Distributed LLM Inference: A Systems Synthesis and Research Agenda for an Energy-, Carbon-, and Cache-Aware llm-d Control Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05565)
*Twinkll Sisodia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文主张将大语言模型推理的可持续性视为分布式推理控制平面的系统性优化问题，而不是仅依赖模型训练或单一硬件改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中，LLM 的能耗、碳排放和成本不仅由模型规模决定，还受到工作负载形态、批处理、KV cache 复用、prefill/decode 放置、模型与加速器选择、功耗状态、地域碳强度和 SLO 等多因素影响。

Method: 论文采用文献综合方法，将近期系统研究归纳为若干重复出现的设计模式，并据此提出面向 llm-d 的 Sustainable Inference Control Plane（SICP）构想，同时设计基于 SLO 满足条件下每焦耳 goodput 和每克 CO2e goodput 的评估框架与可复现实验方案。

Result: 论文提出控制平面应在满足 TTFT/TPOT 等硬约束 SLO 的前提下，综合延迟、能耗、碳排放、缓存复用、服务成本和生成质量，在模型、推理阶段、缓存、硬件、副本、区域和时间等维度进行路由与扩缩容决策。

Conclusion: 可持续 LLM 推理更可能来自跨层、跨资源的控制优化，而不是依赖某个“绿色”模型或某一种加速器；未来需要系统性控制平面和标准化评估来协调性能、成本与环境影响。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) sustainability is increasingly a serving-systems problem, not only a training problem. In production, energy and carbon impact depend on more than model size: workload shape, batching, key-value (KV) cache reuse, prefill/decode placement, model and accelerator choice, power state, geographic carbon intensity, and service-level objectives (SLOs) all matter. Recent systems papers study many of these factors separately. This paper connects those results and asks a practical engineering question: what do they imply when the decision point is a distributed inference control plane such as llm-d?
  The contribution here is synthesis, not a new set of benchmark results. Reported performance, energy, carbon, and cost improvements remain the results of the cited papers and systems. I group the literature into recurring design patterns and use those patterns to sketch a Sustainable Inference Control Plane (SICP) for llm-d. The proposed control plane would consider latency, energy, carbon, cache reuse, serving cost, and quality when routing and scaling, while keeping TTFT/TPOT SLOs as hard constraints. I also outline an evaluation framework based on SLO-satisfied goodput per joule and per gram CO2e, together with a reproducible experimental plan. The main observation from connecting the literature is that sustainable LLM inference is unlikely to come from one "green" model or one accelerator; it is more naturally treated as a control problem across model, phase, cache, hardware, replica, region, and time.

</details>


### [130] [Sharpedo: Dual-Mode Uncertified DAG-Based Consensus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05580)
*Zeno de Angeli*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Sharpedo is the first dual-mode uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) consensus protocol that combines high performance from partially-synchronous protocols with strong progress guarantees in asynchronous settings, along with a dynamic scheduling mechanism for improved efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing consensus protocols like Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi face trade-offs between network assumption flexibility and performance. While Mysticeti optimizes for low latency under timing assumptions but sacrifices robustness, Mahi-Mahi prioritizes progress guarantees in asynchronous environments at the cost of higher latency. No existing dual-mode protocol fully leverages the advantages of the new uncertified DAG data structure across different network assumptions.

Method: The research traces the evolution of consensus protocols, analyzes how they handle network assumption constraints, and develops Sharpedo as a dual-mode protocol. It introduces a novel dynamic scheduling mechanism integrated into the DAG-based consensus framework.

Result: Sharpedo achieves the best of both worlds by combining the high performance of partially-synchronous protocols with guaranteed progress in asynchronous settings. The protocol also incorporates a dynamic scheduling mechanism that improves system efficiency and overall performance optimization.

Conclusion: Sharpedo represents a significant advancement in distributed systems by unifying dual-mode operation through its innovative approach to DAG-based consensus, addressing the limitations of previous protocols and providing a more flexible and efficient solution for various network conditions.

Abstract: Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi represent leading approaches to consensus protocols, leveraging a novel uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph data structure to achieve substantial performance benefits compared to prior work. Mysticeti and Mahi-Mahi differ in their underlying network assumptions, where the former makes assumptions about timing bounds for communication while the latter does not. Mysticeti trades robustness for lower levels of latency in ideal conditions, whereas Mahi-Mahi favours increased latency in order to ensure stronger progress guarantees in asynchronous settings. Although dual-mode protocols exist (i.e., protocols that can adapt do different network assumptions), none have been implemented to take full advantage of the novel data structure seen in the aforementioned protocols. Thus, our research project aims to trace the progression of consensus protocols, analyse how they address network assumptions constraints, and explore dual-mode protocols. Then, we present Sharpedo, the first dual-mode uncertified Directed Acyclic Graph consensus protocols which aims to achieve the best of both worlds: the high-performance of partially-synchronous protocols, while providing guarantees of progress even in asynchronous settings. Furthermore, Sharpedo integrates a novel dynamic scheduling mechanism resulting in improved system efficiency and performance optimization.

</details>


### [131] [CoCoFL_Continual_Computing_for_Federated_Learning_over_Intermittent_Satellite-Ground_Links](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05997)
*Yun Shen,Kun Guo,Xi Yang,Yaoqi Liu,Yisheng Zhao,Wei Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出CoCoFL框架，用于低轨卫星星座辅助的联邦学习，在部分设备可通信的可见窗口内提升设备参与率和训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星场景中，卫星与地面设备之间的间歇性链路导致每轮只能有少量设备参与聚合，未被调度的设备及其本地数据、计算资源被闲置；同时数据异构会使全局模型偏向已参与设备，影响学习性能。

Method: 提出持续计算型联邦学习框架CoCoFL：已调度设备参与全局聚合，未调度设备不等待，而是继续本地更新并考虑模型陈旧性。根据收敛分析，并在可见窗口时间约束下，联合优化设备调度策略以及已调度/未调度设备的本地训练轮数。

Result: 实验表明，相比基线方法，CoCoFL能够更快收敛，降低训练损失，并提高测试准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过让未调度设备持续本地学习，缓解了卫星联邦学习中部分设备参与造成的资源闲置和偏差问题，为低轨卫星辅助联邦学习提供了更高效的调度与训练优化方案。

Abstract: Low earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations enable geographically distributed ground devices to collaboratively train a global model via federated learning (FL) without sharing raw data, with applications in environmental monitoring and disaster prediction. However, in satellite-assisted FL scenarios, intermittent satellite-ground links allow only a subset of devices to participate in global aggregation within each visibility window, leaving unscheduled devices idle and their local computational and data resources underutilized. Under partial device participation, data heterogeneity among devices may bias the global model toward certain devices, thereby deteriorating learning performance. In this regard, we propose a continual computing based federated learning framework, referred to as CoCoFL, in which scheduled devices participate in the global model aggregation, while unscheduled devices continue updating their local models taking into account model staleness. Guided by the convergence analysis of CoCoFL and subject to visible-window-related time constraints, we jointly optimize the device scheduling and the number of local epochs for scheduled and unscheduled devices. Experimental results demonstrate that CoCoFL achieves faster convergence, lower training loss, and higher test accuracy compared with baselines.

</details>


### [132] [EStream: Fast and Memory-Efficient MoE Prefill through Expert Virtualization on Mobile NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06551)
*Junming Zhang,Zhenzhe Zheng,Fan Wu,Xiaoyao Huang,Jie Wu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EStream 通过软硬件协同设计，将 NPU 编译时固定图与 MoE 运行时专家选择解耦，利用 UFS 闪存虚拟化和分页机制结合硬件感知的 pipeline 配置，实现移动端 MoE 预填无 padding 纯 NPU 执行。在覆盖 7B-16B、256-4096 token 设置的 18 组实验中，相比最快 baseline，EStream 实现 2.25-27.57× TTFT 加速并将峰值物理内存降低 1.19-12.29×，成功支撑高达 46.7B 参数的 MoE 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动端 LLM 系统多依赖稠密模型，稀疏 MoE 模型因运行时专家选择与 NPU 编译图固定的冲突，难以在移动 NPU 上高效部署，且专家池规模超出单设备内存上限，限制了大模型部署。

Method: EStream 通过编译一个固定专家图，在调用时动态绑定每个专家的路由 Token 与权重地址，实现 MoE 完全在 NPU 上执行。同时利用 UFS 闪存作为专家池，通过固定大小 NPU 可寻址 arena 进行分组分页加载，并将数据加载隐藏在计算后延迟；进一步提出硬件感知的配置算法以最大化加载与计算的重叠。

Result: 在 18 种涉及三款 7B-16B MoE 模型与 256-4096 token 提示的比较设置中，EStream 在商业 Snapdragon 手机上相比各设置下最快的 baseline，实现纯预填 TTFT 2.25-27.57× 加速，峰值物理内存降低 1.19-12.29×，并成功扩展至 46.7B 参数的 MoE 模型。

Conclusion: EStream 通过编译时图固定、运行时地址绑定、UFS 分页加载及硬件感知 pipeline 配置，有效突破了移动端 MoE 预填的内存与计算瓶颈，展示了在主流移动硬件上大规模 MoE 模型高效部署的可行性。

Abstract: Mobile vendors and application developers increasingly deploy LLMs on smartphones for diverse prefill-only services. Yet current systems rely mainly on dense models whose regular computation maps efficiently to mobile NPUs, leaving more capable MoEs underused. MoE prefill does not fit mobile NPUs: NPU graphs are fixed at compile time, yet MoE picks experts at runtime; and one request touches most experts, more than a phone can hold in memory. We present EStream, which resolves both by separating what the NPU must fix from what MoE decides at runtime. A single compiled expert graph serves every expert, with each expert's routed tokens and weight address bound at call time, so dynamic MoE execution runs entirely on the NPU without padding or CPU/GPU fallback. Expert virtualization keeps the expert pool in UFS flash storage and pages it through a fixed-size NPU-addressable arena, group by group, with loading hidden behind computation, so memory is bounded by the arena rather than by the model. It further introduces a hardware-aware configuration algorithm that automatically configures the UFS--NPU pipeline and maximizes loading--computation overlap. Across 18 comparative settings covering three 7B--16B MoEs and 256--4,096-token prompts, we evaluate EStream on a commercial Snapdragon smartphone. Compared to the fastest baseline at each setting, EStream achieves a 2.25--27.57X pure-prefill TTFT speedup and reduces peak physical memory by 1.19--12.29X. EStream further scales to MoE models with up to 46.7B parameters.

</details>


### [133] [Poseidon: DAG-Guided Parallelism Search for LLM Pre-Training on Heterogeneous Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06086)
*Xiaosong Chen,Shaoheng Nie,Zhongmin Zhao,Zizhao Mo,Jiapeng Chen,Huanle Xu,Zeren Li,Weiwei Sun,ChengZhong Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Poseidon框架通过显式训练时间模型和两种理论驱动的策略，显著提升异构集群上大语言模型的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在异构加速集群上预训练大语言模型时，训练时间模型不准确，导致并行优化受阻，且配置搜索空间大，难以全面探索。

Method: Poseidon基于有向环形图的显式训练时间模型，并采用阶段级剪枝（提前停止部分估计）和层到阶段的映射（利用山脊分布模式）两种策略，大幅缩小搜索空间而不牺牲效率。

Result: 在异构集群实验表明，Poseidon相较于最先进系统可提升训练吞吐量最高2.76倍。

Conclusion: 通过准确的时间建模和高效的搜索策略，Poseidon实现了在异构加速环境下的可扩展、高效的LLM预训练。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of accelerator technologies, pre-training large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous accelerator clusters has become increasingly crucial for maximizing hardware utilization. Existing systems, however, suffer from inaccurate training time modeling, which undermines the parallelization optimizations built upon it. Moreover, for current approaches, the vast configuration search space makes exhaustive exploration infeasible, forcing a trade-off between search time and training efficiency.
  To overcome these limitations, we introduce Poseidon, an efficient and scalable LLM training framework designed with heterogeneity awareness. Its core is an explicit training time model based on a directed acyclic graph. Building on this graph, Poseidon employs two efficient, theoretically grounded strategies: stage-level pruning via early stopping with partial estimation, and layer-to-stage mapping exploiting a ridge-like distribution pattern. These strategies reduce the search space without sacrificing optimal training efficiency. Experiments on heterogeneous clusters show that Poseidon improves training throughput by up to $2.76\times$ over state-of-the-art systems.

</details>


### [134] [Gutenberg: Taming Latency-Critical Cloud Services with Near-Data-Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06691)
*Qiushi Lin,Phillip B. Gibbons,Jovan Stojkovic,Yiwei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Gutenberg is a CPU+Near-Data Processing (NDP) system designed for latency-critical cloud services that addresses memory pressure, isolation, fairness, and predictable QoS by staging subpage updates in a CPU-resident delta buffer and using CPU helper cores to handle costly operations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Latency-critical cloud services face growing pressure on memory while requiring isolation, fairness, and predictable quality of service. While near-data processing (NDP) reduces data movement by executing requests close to memory, write operations make replica maintenance expensive, and uneven compute/memory traffic can overload NDP units, increasing tail latency. Traditional throughput-oriented schedulers fail to adequately address these challenges for co-located cloud services.

Method: Gutenberg implements a CPU+near-data processing architecture where subpage updates are staged in a CPU-resident delta buffer, keeping hot writable pages replicated without eager full-page synchronization. It employs CPU helper cores to assist request execution during costly NDP operations or replica maintenance. An online controller jointly optimizes page placement, replication strategy, CPU/NDP execution assignment, and routing based on access patterns, queue pressure, and feedback from previous decisions. The system enforces isolation and fair resource allocation across services, and includes formal models for verifying CPU-NDP coordination correctness.

Result: Evaluated on TailBench using ZSim with Ramulator-calibrated memory timing, Gutenberg achieves significant performance improvements: average latency reduced by up to 80.4% and p99 latency by up to 85.8%. It also enhances isolation and fairness while maintaining adaptability to changing workload behaviors.

Conclusion: Gutenberg successfully demonstrates that combining CPU-assisted near-data processing with intelligent online scheduling can effectively reduce latency and improve fairness in latency-critical cloud services, making it a promising approach for next-generation cloud computing architectures.

Abstract: Latency-critical cloud services place growing pressure on memory while requiring isolation, fairness, and predictable QoS. Near-data processing (NDP) reduces data movement by executing requests close to memory, and prior systems further improve locality through caching and replication. However, writes make replica maintenance expensive, while uneven compute and memory traffic can overload a few NDP units and increase tail latency. Existing throughput-oriented schedulers do not fully address these challenges for co-located cloud services.
  We present Gutenberg, a CPU+NDP for mutable, latency-critical cloud services. Gutenberg stages subpage updates in a CPU-resident delta buffer, allowing hot writable pages to remain replicated without eager full-page synchronization. It also adopts CPU helper cores to assist request execution when NDP execution or replica maintenance becomes costly. An online controller jointly decides page placement, replication, CPU/NDP execution, and routing using access patterns, queue pressure, and feedback from prior decisions. The system further enforces isolation and fair resource allocation across services. We also model-check CPU--NDP coordination protocol for correctness.
  We evaluate on TailBench using ZSim with Ramulator-calibrated memory timing. Across evaluated services, Gutenberg outperforms prior systems, reducing average and p99 latency by up to 80.4% and 85.8%. It also improves isolation and fairness while adapting to changing workload behaviors.

</details>


### [135] [Diamond Agent: Agentic Control of Federated HPC Resources as a Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06181)
*Haotian Xie,Junlin Chen,Mingkai Zheng,Yifan Zhu,Minu Mathew,Max Burnette,Yadu Babuji,Volodymyr Kindratenko,Shivaram Venkataraman,Kyle Chard,Ian Foster,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了Diamond Agent，一个以类型化技能为接口的智能体系统，用于在异构集群中智能执行HPC工作流。它通过提供统一的工作空间和技能，结合事件驱动的延续机制，实现了跨站点的资源发现、数据移动和高效任务调度，在真实多站点测试中将额外完成时间中位数降低了10.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构集群间聚合和编排计算能力以执行HPC工作流面临四个实际挑战：跨独立管理集群保持工作流工作上下文、在站点间移动大型数据集、推理特定站点的环境和调度策略，以及利用实时队列和资源状态进行高效任务调度。

Method: 设计了Diamond Agent系统，提供智能体 facing 工作空间和技能，统一跨站点资源发现、资源规范、数据移动、任务执行和结果检索。中心化实例可操作多个超级计算机，利用Globus Transfer移动数据，并根据实时系统能力和队列信息选择最佳放置位置。其事件驱动的延续机制通过持久化服务监控远程执行，将智能体动作与长时间运行的批处理任务解耦，仅在关键事件发生时恢复智能体。

Result: 在四个生产级超级计算机上进行了27小时的遥测和19轮匹配的多站点提交（共83个作业）实验。与固定站点基线相比，Diamond Agent将相对最快观察放置的中位额外完成时间从42秒减少到4秒，实现了10.5倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: Diamond Agent通过智能体技术和事件驱动架构，有效解决了异构HPC集群工作流编排中的核心挑战，显著减少了任务调度和执行的时间开销，实现了高效的跨集群计算资源聚合。

Abstract: Efficiently aggregating and orchestrating computing power across heterogeneous clusters for HPC workflows faces four practical challenges: preserving workflow context across independently administered clusters, moving large datasets between sites, reasoning about site-specific environments and scheduler policies, and exploiting live queue and resource states for efficient task scheduling. To this end, we design Diamond Agent, an agentic system that enables intelligent execution of HPC workflows across heterogeneous clusters with typed skills as the interface. Diamond Agent provides an agent-facing workspace and skills that unify cross-site resource discovery, resource specification, data movement, task execution, and result retrieval. A centralized Diamond Agent instance can operate multiple supercomputers without being deployed separately on each login node. Diamond Agent translates high-level agent actions into valid site-specific executions, moves data through Globus Transfer, and uses live system capability and queue information to select feasible placements. Its event-driven continuation mechanism decouples agent actions from long-running batch jobs: persistent services monitor remote execution and resume the agent only when a result or decision-relevant event is available. We experiment with 27 hours of telemetry and 19 matched multi-site submission rounds comprising 83 jobs across four production supercomputers. Compared with a fixed-site baseline, Diamond Agent reduces the median additional completion time relative to the fastest observed placement from 42 seconds to 4 seconds, a 10.5x reduction.

</details>


### [136] [Water-network decisions share one hydraulic gradient, and it can now be computed exactly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06323)
*Tianwei Mu,Yue Wang,Mingzhe Yuan,Wenhong Wang,Qing Luo,Min Xiao,Jun Li,Hui Yang,Manhong Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: The paper presents a fully differentiable reformulation of the Global Gradient Algorithm (GGA) for water distribution networks, enabling efficient gradient-based optimization via automatic differentiation by reusing the forward pass factorization for adjoint solves on a GPU. Validated across 52 networks with 8,140 frames, achieving 1.137e-13 ft head deviation vs EPANET 2.2, it replaces 906 finite-difference simulations with one adjoint solve and accelerates leak-inversion training by 191x.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hydraulic simulation engines like EPANET return solutions but not derivatives, forcing practitioners to rely on computationally expensive derivative-free methods or surrogate models that inherit modeling errors. This limits the scalability of gradient-based calibration, leak localization, and sensor placement tasks in water distribution networks.

Method: We make the Global Gradient Algorithm (GGA) exactly differentiable. The forward pass replicates reference engine behaviors including discrete devices, status switching, and low-flow linearization. The backward pass computes gradients via an implicit adjoint method that reuses the terminal factorization from the forward pass. All computations are batched over scenarios and executed on a single GPU. We evaluate across 52 public, synthetic, and operational water distribution networks covering 8,140 simulation frames, comparing against EPANET 2.2.

Result: Every network meets the acceptance criterion with a maximum head deviation of 1.137e-13 ft from EPANET 2.2, and 25 networks match exactly. One adjoint solve replaces 906 finite-difference roughness Jacobian simulations on the 905-pipe L-TOWN benchmark. A leak-inversion training loop runs in 463-470 ms per optimizer step for 256 scenarios — 191 times faster than the prior pipeline. Gradient calibration reaches its endpoint within a median of 595 model calls, while the strongest of five tuned metaheuristics requires 8,060 calls to match the training loss, and two fail to converge within 20,000 calls. On a 554-link operating network, one adjoint pass audits sensor-constrained roughness parameters and identifies optimal sensor additions.

Conclusion: By rendering the Global Gradient Algorithm differentiable and leveraging GPU-accelerated adjoint solves, the method enables scalable, accurate, and fast gradient-based optimization for water distribution network applications including calibration, leak inversion, and sensor placement. This represents a significant advancement over derivative-free and surrogate-based approaches in terms of both accuracy and computational efficiency, demonstrated across diverse real-world and synthetic benchmarks.

Abstract: Calibration, leak localisation and sensor placement on water distribution networks (WDNs) are decisions about continuous parameters, yet the hydraulic engine that defines the physics returns a solution and no derivatives, so practice falls back on derivative-free search or on surrogates whose error the answer inherits. We make the global gradient algorithm itself exactly differentiable: the forward pass reproduces the reference engine's discrete devices, status switching and low-flow linearisation included, and the backward pass solves the implicit adjoint by reusing the forward pass's terminal factorisation, so one extra sparse solve returns every parameter's gradient at once, batched over scenarios on one graphics processor. Across 52 public, synthetic and operational networks and 8,140 simulation frames, every network meets the acceptance criterion, the largest head deviation from EPANET 2.2 is 1.137e-13 ft and 25 agree exactly. One adjoint solve replaces the 906 simulations a finite-difference roughness Jacobian costs on the 905-pipe L-TOWN benchmark, and a leak-inversion training loop runs at 463-470 ms per optimiser step for 256 scenarios, 191 times the prior pipeline. Gradient calibration reaches its endpoint within a median 595 model calls, where the strongest of five tuned metaheuristics needs 8,060 to match it on the training loss and two never do within 20,000. On a 554-link operating network, one adjoint pass audits, pipe by pipe, which roughness parameters the installed sensors can constrain and which sensors to add, on the model the utility already operates.

</details>


### [137] [Cops and Lethal Robber on Rings: A Distributed Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06388)
*Amanpreet Singh Saini,Ashish Saxena,Kaushik Mondal*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在环形网络上研究了一种致命强盗变体（致命强盗），证明仅用 n 名警察无法保证捕获，并提出了一种从任意初始布局出发、最坏情况下需要 n + ⌊log n⌋ + 4 名警察的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了将经典的警察与强盗游戏推广到分布式环境，并赋予强盗杀死警察的能力，从而将该问题与入侵者捕获和黑洞搜索等动态实体问题联系起来。

Method: 在静态环形图（大小为 n）上定义警察与致命强盗问题；先给出下界证明：即使所有警察起始于同一顶点，n 名警察也不足以保证捕获；然后设计一种算法，利用额外的 ⌊log n⌋+4 名警察在任意初始配置下实现捕获。

Result: 下界：n 名警察不足以在最坏情况下捕获致命强盗；上界：所提出的算法在最坏情况下使用 n + ⌊log n⌋ + 4 名警察即可成功捕获。

Conclusion: 致命强盗显著提高了捕获难度，在环形网络上需要比经典情况多 O(log n) 名警察；这为分布式场景下的致命实体捕获问题提供了初步结果，并为进一步研究其他拓扑和更强的强盗能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Cops and Robber game is extensively studied in the sequential setting where the main goal is to capture the robber. Capturing the robber means at least one cop, and the robber will be at the same vertex together at some time. There are several variants, including variants where the goal of the cops is to surround the robber. Surrounding the robber means there is at least one cop in each of the neighboring vertices of the robber's position. In this paper, we introduce it in the distributed setting while empowering the robber by saying it can even kill cops. Specifically, in our model, the robber moves in odd rounds and has unbounded speed, cops move in even rounds, and if one or more cops move into a vertex where the robber is currently residing, all these cops get killed. We call this {\it lethal robber}. This also connects our work to the Intruder Capture and Black Hole Search problems by introducing an entity which is dynamic as well as lethal, a setting that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been studied.
  In this work, we introduce the lethal robber, define the {\it cops and lethal robber} problem in the distributed setting and study it on a static ring of size $n$. We prove $n$ cops are not enough, even if all start from the same vertex, and provide an algorithm starting from an arbitrary initial configuration that requires $n+\lfloor\log n \rfloor+4$ cops in the worst case.

</details>


### [138] [ForgeStencil: Automating Per-Case Stencil Specialization from Kernels to 100+ Real Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.06694)
*Yaojian Chen,Yuxuan Li,Wubing Wan,Lin Gan,Guangwen Yang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ForgeStencil采用代码合成Agent为每个 stencil 配置合成专用CUDA kernel，在A100上达到与SOTA基线相当或更优的性能，端到端应用中位数加速1.41x，并且所有结果均经过测量完整性校验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Stencil DSL、代码生成器和自动调优工具追求通用性，难以适应不同stencil形状、精度和应用场景的差异化需求；而人工为每个案例专设内核成本过高，不可扩展。ForgeStencil质疑这一假设，认为代码合成Agent已降低了逐案定制的成本，使得为每个配置构建专用解决方案成为可能。
